Equally important, the gains do not appear to come with a major collapse in general capability. The paper reports preserved performance on GPQA, MMLU-Pro, AIME, and IFEval, which matters because long-context fine-tuning often causes capability drift.

The most informative ablations are the ones that separate ACC from standard agent SFT, showing the benefit comes from supervision restructuring rather than simply exposing the model to more tokens. The paper also compares different agent sources and includes mechanism-level analyses of attention and expert behavior. The abstract does not expose the full data scale, so for reproduction the key missing details to extract from the full text are trajectory counts by domain, compiled context lengths, filtering/verification retention rates, and the exact training token budget.4c:T8e6,技术上，作者把 synthetic user experiment 放进因果推断框架里分析。设 A 是 intervention，Y 是主要 outcome，X 是完整 persona 属性，L 是显式写进 prompt 的观测属性，其余 X\L 是潜在属性。理想随机试验要求 treatment assignment 与潜在结果独立，或者至少 treatment 不应改变 treatment 之前就固定的用户属性分布。但在 LLM simulation 中，模型会根据不同 intervention 对未指定属性做不同补全，于是条件分布 P(X\L | A, L) 会随 A 改变。这样即便初始 persona prompt 相同，实际生成 outcome 的“用户”也变了，形成 selection bias / confounding。

文章的一个关键贡献是把这种 drift 变成可诊断对象。作者引入 negative control outcomes：这些变量在真实实验中应当不受 intervention 影响，而且在时间上、因果上先于 intervention，例如 race、出生背景等固定属性。如果在 synthetic experiment 中，不同 intervention 下这些 negative controls 的分布发生系统性变化，就说明 intervention 改变了模型内部模拟出来的用户群体，而不是只改变了用户对 intervention 的反应。这个诊断思路很干净，因为它不要求知道真实 treatment effect，只要求找到理论上不应被 treatment 改变的属性。

在缓解方面，作者测试了 confounder adjustment during persona specification。直观上，就是在 persona prompt 里补充更多与 treatment 和 outcome 都相关的属性，减少模型自由补全的空间。论文的结论不是“属性越多越好”，而是要补那些与实验机制直接相关的 confounders。若加入与 treatment/outcome 关系弱的属性，收益不稳定，甚至可能引入新的偏移。这一点很重要，因为它说明 drift 不是简单靠 prompt 变长就能解决，而是要做因果上有针对性的 persona 设计。

从 LLM 机制角度看，这篇文章抓住了一个生成模型常见现象：当 prompt 不充分时，模型会用训练语料中的相关性结构去补全世界状态。用于创作时这是能力，用于因果实验时却会破坏识别条件。作者把这个现象从直觉层面推进到了 formal bias analysis，这部分是全文最有价值的地方。4d:T8b6,The paper casts synthetic user experiments in a causal inference framework. Let A be the intervention, Y the primary outcome, X the full user persona, and L the observed subset of persona attributes explicitly specified in the prompt. The remaining attributes X\L are latent. In a valid randomized experiment, treatment assignment should not alter pre-treatment user attributes. But in LLM simulations, the model may infer different latent personas under different interventions, so the conditional distribution P(X\L | A, L) changes with A. That creates selection bias: even if the visible persona is the same, the effective simulated user is not.

The main diagnostic tool is the use of negative control outcomes. These are user attributes that should remain invariant under intervention and are causally upstream of it, such as race or other fixed background characteristics. If their distribution changes across treatment conditions in the synthetic experiment, that is evidence that the intervention has shifted the latent user population rather than merely changing the user’s response. This is a clean idea because it does not require access to ground-truth treatment effects; it only requires attributes that should not be affected by treatment.

For mitigation, the paper studies confounder adjustment at the persona-specification stage. The intuition is to reduce the model’s freedom to invent latent traits by explicitly including attributes that are relevant to both treatment and outcome. An important result is that more persona detail is not automatically better. Adjustment helps when the added attributes are causally relevant to the experimental mechanism. Adding weakly related attributes gives inconsistent benefits and can even worsen bias. That is a useful practical point: the issue is not prompt length, but targeted control of latent confounders.

At a deeper level, the paper is about a general property of generative models. When prompts are underspecified, the model fills in missing world state using correlations learned from observational data. That behavior is often desirable in generation, but it breaks identification assumptions in causal experiments. The paper makes that intuition formal.4e:T640,The experiments are designed to establish three things: user drift exists, negative controls can detect it, and targeted confounder adjustment can reduce it. The authors report evidence of confounding across multiple major open-source and proprietary LLM families, which suggests the problem is not model-specific but tied to the generative interface itself.

The empirical setup appears to have three main components. First, synthetic intervention experiments where the same or similar initial persona is exposed to different agents or treatments, while both the primary outcome and one or more negative control attributes are measured. If the negative controls differ systematically across treatments, that indicates drift. Second, bias-direction analysis showing that intervention-dependent drift can either inflate or attenuate the observed treatment effect. This matters because the issue is not just extra variance; it can change the sign or magnitude of the estimated effect. Third, mitigation experiments where additional confounders are included in the persona prompt. Relevant confounders reduce selection bias, while less relevant ones help inconsistently and may even make things worse.

The paper is more about effect patterns than a single headline metric. The most informative details are likely the size of negative-control shifts, consistency across model families, and which confounders actually help. If the paper reports treatment-effect estimates before and after adjustment, those comparisons are especially important because they show how unstable synthetic conclusions can be.4f:T45c,The paper proposes a collapse-and-refine mechanism and instantiates it as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD):
- Stage 1 (low-noise, manifold learning): due to the score’s singular behavior at small noise, the induced denoising map rapidly collapses onto a projection to the data manifold. Using mean-field gradient flow analysis for two-layer networks, they show exponential decay of a geometric alignment risk.
- Stage 2 (moderate-noise, density-on-manifold learning): once geometry is captured, training refines the intrinsic density. They analyze this via random-feature regression on the manifold, deriving generalization bounds where excess risk scales polynomially with k and sample size n, independent of d.
- End-to-end sampling guarantee: decompose error into a manifold-regime term (k-dependent) and a high-noise term (polynomial in d but exponentially damped by integration time), avoiding the ambient curse.
Implementation-wise, SiLD keeps a single DDPM objective—no auxiliary losses—and argues the latent representation is induced by the score itself rather than imposed by an external encoder.50:T81a,The paper has two main technical pieces. First is d_NTP, a discrepancy measure between the next-token distributions produced by full ICL and by task-vector-based inference. The key claim is that downstream accuracy is only an indirect signal, while distributional alignment is a more faithful criterion because task vectors are supposed to reproduce the effect of demonstrations, not merely match the final argmax label on average. Empirically, lower d_NTP correlates strongly with better downstream performance.

Second is Linear Task Vector (LTV). Instead of extracting a vector by simple hidden-state differencing or other heuristics, LTV models the effect of demonstrations as a linear transformation on the query representation. The method sets up a regression problem between representations with and without demonstrations and solves it in closed form. The extracted task vector is therefore tied to an explicit approximation objective rather than an ad hoc construction. The novelty is not linearity by itself; the point is that the extraction procedure is derived from a distribution-alignment perspective.

Mechanistically, LTV assumes that in classification settings, much of the useful effect of demonstrations can be approximated as a roughly linear shift in the final hidden representation relevant to label prediction. That is clearly an approximation, since ICL is not globally linear, but it can still be effective when the label space is small and the decision boundary is local. The paper also extends the analysis beyond classification to regression, suggesting that the method is not merely exploiting label-token quirks.

A notable extension is transferability: task vectors extracted from a larger model are applied to smaller models. This is interesting because it implicitly tests whether the task effect learned through ICL has some shared geometric structure across models. The reported gains suggest that at least part of that structure transfers, which is practically useful for smaller models with weaker ICL ability or shorter context windows.51:T5d2,The experiments cover 8 classification benchmarks and 5 LLMs, with additional results on regression tasks. The headline number in the paper is that LTV improves average classification accuracy by 9.2% over prior task-vector baselines while also reducing inference latency relative to full ICL, since demonstrations are removed at test time. Another central empirical result is the strong negative correlation between d_NTP and downstream accuracy, which is the main evidence that the proposed metric is meaningful.

The empirical story has four parts. First, direct method comparison: LTV is more consistently effective than existing task-vector extraction methods across models and datasets. Second, metric validation: methods with lower d_NTP tend to perform better, supporting the idea that distributional alignment is a useful design criterion. Third, task generalization: the method also outperforms baselines on regression, which weakens the concern that it only works in narrow label-token classification setups. Fourth, cross-model transfer: using task vectors extracted from a larger model improves smaller-model classification accuracy by 6.4%.

The most informative details are likely in the per-model and per-task breakdowns rather than the average alone. In particular, cases where d_NTP improves more than accuracy would be worth inspecting, because they may indicate that the task vector is becoming more faithful to ICL even when ICL itself is not the task-optimal strategy.52:T457,The framework defines a ground-truth answer map, a hypothesis answer map, and a chain rule that maps current question-answer states to the next question. For recoverable inputs, the ground-truth trajectory can recover the final answer. Under a quasimetric loss assumption, the reasoning risk decomposes into two terms. Oracle-trajectory risk measures the hypothesis error when the trajectory is guided by the ground truth up to the final step. Trajectory-mismatch risk measures the extra error caused by the hypothesis driving the chain rule along a different trajectory. The oracle term is connected to domain adaptation: the ground-truth CoT trajectory pushes the test distribution into a target distribution, and CoT helps when that distribution is close to the training distribution. The mismatch term captures the cost of self-generated reasoning. The paper proves that without stability assumptions on the loss, the answer map, and the chain rule, this cost can be arbitrarily large. With stability, it gives an optimal upper bound with bounded, linear, and exponential regimes in the number of CoT steps.53:T418,Starting from the known algebraic identity between single-head softmax attention and the Nadaraya–Watson (NW) estimator, the paper shows MHA is a structured ensemble of H NW estimators, each operating in a different learned key-projection subspace Range(W_K^h), followed by aggregation.
Main theoretical components:
- An exact MSE Bias–Variance–Covariance decomposition for MHA, highlighting that inter-head covariance drives statistical efficiency.
- A Head Diversity Index (HDI) that summarizes decorrelation via principal angles between key subspaces; practically computed from singular values of the cross-Gram matrix G_{hh'}=(W_K^h)^T W_K^{h'}/d_k.
- A decorrelation-optimality result: MSE-minimizing projections satisfy approximate orthogonality (W_K^h)^T W_K^{h'}≈0 for h≠h'.
- An “optimal architecture” result under H·d_k=D, yielding a theory-derived optimum (H*, d_k*).
- A bound suggesting non-uniform (e.g., geometrically decaying) head weights can outperform uniform averaging when heads have different consistency rates.54:T7df,The method has two key layers. First is the modeling choice: memory is a separate parametric policy π_mem rather than a memory bank. The input is the current context x=(q,o), where q is the task specification and o is the environment observation. The output is either a short textual guidance m inserted into the downstream agent context, or an abstention decision. This decouples specialization from execution: a smaller model can specialize as memory, while a larger frontier agent remains the executor.

Training is done in two stages. Stage one is experience distillation. The authors build an offline experience bank E={(x,m)} and train π_mem with supervised learning to reproduce reusable guidance from context. This compresses the experience bank into the memory model parameters. Stage two is adaptation distillation, where reinforcement learning optimizes the memory policy directly against downstream task outcomes.

The main technical point is the decision-content decoupled objective built on GRPO. For the same context, the training procedure constructs structured counterfactual rollouts that compare at least two branches: generate guidance versus abstain. Within the generate branch, different candidate guidance contents are also compared. This lets the learning signal be decomposed into a decision-level advantage for whether memory should be generated, and a content-level advantage for what the guidance should say. Without this decomposition, a standard sequence-level policy gradient would conflate two failure modes: generating when it should not, and generating the wrong content when it should. That decomposition is the most important idea in the paper.

From a systems perspective, Mem-π is not an always-on helper. It is a selective intervention policy with abstention as a reliability mechanism. The paper explicitly argues that under weak grounding, ambiguity, or OOD contexts, bad generated memory can harm the downstream agent. That makes abstention central rather than optional.55:T60a,The evaluation spans four agent benchmarks: WebArena, WorkArena, LifelongAgentBench, and ALFWorld, covering web navigation, enterprise workflow tasks, terminal tool use, and text-based embodied environments. The headline result is that adaptive memory generation consistently beats retrieval-based memory baselines on all four benchmarks, with roughly 20% average relative improvement over the base agent and nearly 50% relative gain on WebArena. The pattern suggests that generated memory is especially useful when tasks are long-horizon, multi-stage, and strongly context-dependent, where experience recomposition matters more than nearest-neighbor reuse.

The most informative ablations are likely threefold. First, the two-stage training pipeline: supervised distillation alone can produce plausible guidance, but RL is needed to align memory generation with actual task success. Second, the decision-content decoupling: removing it should lead to over-generation or unstable abstention behavior. Third, comparisons against retrieval memory, always-on generated memory, and the no-memory base agent clarify that the gain comes from selective generation rather than merely adding another model.

The paper emphasizes relative gains more than scale. It is not trying to win by sheer parameter count or data volume. The interesting evidence is in trigger rates, abstention rates, guidance length, and the measured harm from bad guidance. Those details matter more than a single average score if one wants to judge whether the method is reliable.56:T437,The proposed approach is agent just-in-time compilation. A natural-language instruction is translated at runtime into executable code that may contain tool calls, LLM calls, and parallel execution. The system has three main components. JIT-Planner generates multiple code plans in parallel, statically validates them against tool signatures and state invariants, estimates their cost on a control-flow graph, and selects the lowest-cost valid candidate. JIT-Scheduler uses latency distributions collected offline and Monte Carlo estimation to choose among serial execution, parallel execution, and request hedging. The tool protocol requires each tool to declare preconditions and postconditions; a sequence is rejected if one tool’s postcondition does not satisfy the next tool’s precondition. This differs from typical browser agents by lifting the action space from primitive click/type/scroll operations to reusable tool code such as list_restaurants or add_to_cart. LLM calls become optional steps inside a program rather than the default controller at every This is observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures and optimizers and cannot be explained using prior theory. time in MoE training. Four families of system-level mitigations (predictive sample placement, adaptive expert relayout, hierarchical collectives, EP-aware topology) all rest on two untested assumptions: (A) routing imbalance is correctable by the system layer; (B) mock-token benchmarks faithfully represent production routing behavior. This paper empirically tests both assumptions via cross-architecture measurements.","importance":"This is a foundational empirical study in MoE system optimization. Its findings directly challenge the evaluation methodology and basic assumptions of most current MoE scheduling optimization papers. Concrete impacts on pretrain research: (1) EP scaling is not a lever for routing balance—changing which rank holds which expert does not change which experts the model routes tokens to; (2) mock data systematically overestimates routing imbalance, with the gap growing with batch size—meaning conclusions of many systems papers based on synthetic data need re-examination; (3) architecture type (attention/sequence mixer design) has a decisive effect on routing patterns, falling into two categories: data-resilient (MHA, Mamba-2) and persistently concentrated (MLA, GDN). System design should be based on this taxonomy rather than a single workload model.","technical_approach":"Built DODOCO (Dispatch Overhead Diagnostics Observatory) measurement framework, testing 5 MoE architectures × 6 data conditions in a 5×6 factorial design, plus controlled EP scans from 4 to 32 ranks and global batch size sweeps. Ran on 4 H100 nodes (NVLink intra-node, InfiniBand inter-node). All 5 checkpoints use their officially released routing configurations: DeepSeek-V2-Lite (MLA), DeepSeek-MoE-16B (MHA), Qwen3-30B (GQA), Nemotron-30B (Mamba-2), Qwen3.5-35B (GDN). MLA and MHA share identical MoE configurations (64 experts, top-6, same gating/LB), differing only in attention mechanism, forming a controlled pair.","experiments":"Key findings: (Q1) Doubling EP from 16 to 32 leaves per-expert max/mean token ratio essentially flat—MLA varies 5.0%, MHA 2.8%, GDN 0.3%, Mamba-2 4.4%, GQA 4.4%, all ≤5% with no monotonic trend. EP scaling changes expert physical location, not routing preference itself. (Q2) Mock tokens overestimate routing Gini by up to 2.35× at small probe batches, and the gap grows with batch size (mock Gini drifts upward with GBS while real-data Gini is flat); measured by fitted Dirichlet α, the mock-to-real gap reaches 47× on Nemotron. (Q3) Inter-architecture differences are sharp: MHA and Mamba-2 achieve the lowest per-rank Gini under real text (0.105 and 0.150) → data-resilient; MLA and GDN stay above 0.24 even on best real data, rising to 0.29–0.38 on mock → persistently concentrated; GQA is intermediate. The core innovation is that this is the first systematic empirical study testing two fundamental assumptions in the MoE community—the methodology is simple (measurement, not algorithmic innovation), but the findings carry paradigm-shifting implications. Worth digging deeper: why does MLA (DeepSeek's flagship innovation) fall into the persistently concentrated category? This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the qual","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T17:59:40+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21486.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cond-mat.dis-nn","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"大模型训练需要把小模型上调好的优化超参数转移到大模型，但现有做法缺少统一度量。问题不只是“最优学习率是否随宽度不变”，还包括拟合外推是否可靠、外推误差是否会放大、参数化方式是否牺牲最终 loss。该工作进一步追问一个更具体的问题：在 decoder-only Transformer + AdamW 的预训练设置里，μP 相比标准参数化表现出更好的 learning-rate transfer，真正起作用的因素是什么。","importance":"贡献主要在两层。第一层是方法论：给超参数迁移质量定义了可比较的量化框架，避免只看某个尺度上的最优学习率曲线是否平。第二层是训练 recipe：实验显示 μP 的主要收益来自 embedding layer learning rate，而不是 μP 全套参数化改动。对预训练实践的影响很直接。很多大模型训练会用小规模 proxy 搜学习率，再外推到目标规模；如果 embedding 学习率随宽度被压得过低，训练会被输入/输出词表相关参数拖住，甚至出现稳定性问题。这个结论提醒研究者把 embedding learning rate 当成独立的一等超参数，而不是默认跟主体 Transformer block 共用规则。","technical_approach":"技术方案先建立三个指标。第一， scaling-law fit error，用来衡量超参数随规模变化的函数形式是否拟合得好。第二，transfer robustness exponent κ，用来衡量从小规模外推到大规模时，超参数误差对 loss 的影响是否随规模放大或减弱。第三，asymptotic loss degradation R(∞)，用来检查某种参数化虽然迁移更平滑，但是否带来大规模极限下的绝对 loss 损失。随后，作者把标准参数化 SP 和 μP 写到同一个 GPT 预训练框架里，拆出四个差异：embedding 学习率从 Θ(1/n) 变为 Θ(1)，最后一层初始化方差从 Θ(1/n) 变为 Θ(1/n²)，LayerNorm 学习率从 Θ(1/n) 变为 Θ(1)，attention scaling 从 1/√d 变为 1/d。关键实验是对这四个开关做完整 2^4 消融。结果显示，只把 SP 的 embedding learning rate 改成 μP 风格的尺度，就基本能复现 μP 的迁移质量。训练动态分析还显示，embedding 学习率不是只在早期有用；它贯穿训练过程，早期尤其敏感。过小的 embedding 学习率会让表示学习被词嵌入层限制，并诱发训练不稳定。","experiments":"实验集中在 GPT-style decoder-only Transformer 预训练，并使用 AdamW。核心消融覆盖 SP 到 μP 的 4 个差异，共 16 种组合。关键结果是：完整 μP 的 learning-rate transfer 质量可以被“SP + 更大的 embedding layer learning rate”基本匹配；其他改动，如最后一层初始化、LayerNorm 学习率、attention scaling，对该设置下迁移质量的边际贡献较小。工作还报告 warmup 对观察到稳定迁移很重要，说明超参数迁移不能脱离 schedule 讨论。可用材料中没有给出完整参数量、token 数、具体 loss 数值和每个 ablation 的表格数值，因此不宜补写未给出的数字。已有信息足以支持一个明确结论：embedding learning rate 是 μP 在该实验族里迁移效果的主因。","reading_guide":"建议精读三个部分。第一，超参数迁移的三个指标定义，尤其是 κ 和 R(∞)。这部分决定了如何比较不同参数化，而不是只画学习率曲线。第二，SP 与 μP 的统一表述，也就是列出四个差异的表格；这里能看清 μP 在实际 Transformer/AdamW 训练中到底改了哪些量。第三，16 组消融和训练动态分析，重点看 embedding learning rate 单独打开时的曲线、早期训练不稳定现象、warmup 附录。值得深挖的是 embedding 学习率与 tokenizer 词频、输入/输出 embedding tying、AdamW 二阶矩估计、TPP 固定缩放之间的交互。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Large-scale LLM training relies on transferring optimization hyperparameters from small proxy models, but the field lacks a precise way to measure whether such transfer is actually good. The question is broader than whether the optimal learning rate looks width-invariant. A useful transfer scheme should fit a scaling law well, tolerate extrapolation error, and avoid paying an asymptotic loss penalty. The work then asks a more concrete question for decoder-only Transformer pretraining with AdamW: why does μP appear to transfer learning rates better than standard parameterization in practice?","importance":"The contribution is both conceptual and practical. Conceptually, it gives a quantitative framework for comparing hyperparameter-transfer schemes instead of relying on visual flatness of optimal learning-rate curves. Practically, it identifies the embedding-layer learning rate as the dominant factor behind μP-style transfer in the studied GPT pretraining setup. This matters for real pretraining workflows, where learning rates are often tuned on small models and extrapolated. If the embedding learning rate is scaled down too aggressively with width, the embedding layer can become a training bottleneck and a source of instability. The work makes a strong case for treating the embedding learning rate as a separate hyperparameter rather than inheriting the default rule used for the rest of the network.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"The experiments are conducted on GPT-style decoder-only Transformer pretraining with AdamW. The central ablation covers all 16 combinations of the four SP-to-μP differences. The main empirical finding is that full μP can be nearly matched by standard parameterization equipped with an appropriately scaled embedding-layer learning rate. Other changes, including final-layer initialization, LayerNorm learning-rate scaling, and attention scaling, appear less important for transfer quality in this setting. The work also notes that learning-rate warmup is important for observing stable transfer, which means parameterization and schedule cannot be evaluated independently. The provided material does not expose full model sizes, token counts, exact loss values, or the detailed numerical tables, so those numbers should not be inferred.","reading_guide":"The most important parts to read carefully are the metric definitions for hyperparameter transfer, the unified SP-versus-μP parameterization table, and the 16-way ablation section. The robustness exponent κ and asymptotic degradation R(∞) are especially useful if the goal is to design future proxy-to-target tuning protocols. The training-dynamics plots around embedding learning rate and the warmup appendix are also worth close attention. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because invers","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T05:54:24+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20756.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","math.OC","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"预训练常用的带预条件的自适应优化器（AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo这类）的随机更新存在两类未被明确研究的有限样本偏差：一是梯度和预条件器用同一小批量估计带来的耦合偏差，二是预条件器求逆/求逆根的非线性操作带来的逆估计偏差。现有研究将随机更新直接视为群体级预条件下降的近似，忽略了这两类偏差，会影响预训练收敛速度和最终效果。","importance":"明确了自适应优化器设计中之前被忽略的两类统计偏差来源，提出的修正框架可直接嵌入常用优化器，不需要改动核心预训练流程，能在几乎无额外成本的前提下降低预训练困惑度，为优化器设计和预训练调优提供了新的可落地维度。","technical_approach":"首先将实际随机预条件更新与理想群体更新的统计差异分解为前述两类偏差，随后提出单批次修正框架，包含两个核心组件：一是交叉拟合预条件，将当前训练batch拆为独立的微批次组，分别估计梯度和预条件器，消除耦合偏差；二是方差校正求逆，利用微批次间的方差抵消逆操作引入的一阶偏差。该框架分别适配了对角二阶矩、对角曲率、矩阵预条件三类主流优化器。","experiments":"在预训练和指令微调任务上测试，修正后的AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo分别在留出集预训练交叉熵上取得0.1489、0.0701、0.1103 nat的下降，三类优化器均获得一致收益，其中AdamW的提升幅度最大。","reading_guide":"推荐优先阅读第2章问题定义和第3章的两类偏差分解，10分钟左右可掌握核心结论。需要落地实现的读者可参考第4章的三类优化器适配细节，理论推导部分可跳过。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Preconditioned adaptive optimizers widely used in LLM pretraining (e.g., AdamW, Sophia, Shampoo) suffer from two understudied finite-sample biases in their stochastic update rules: coupling bias from estimating gradient and preconditioner on the same minibatch, and inverse estimation bias caused by the nonlinearity of preconditioner inversion or inverse-root operation. Existing work treats stochastic updates as direct approximations of population-level preconditioned descent and ignores these biases, which degrades pretraining convergence and final performance.","importance":"This work explicitly formalizes two previously unaccounted statistical biases in adaptive optimizer design, and provides a drop-in correction framework that can be integrated into common optimizers without modifying core pretraining pipelines. The consistent perplexity reduction across optimizer families offers a new actionable dimension for pretraining optimization tuning.","technical_approach":"First, the work statistically decomposes the mismatch between practical stochastic preconditioned updates and ideal population-level updates into the two aforementioned biases. It then proposes a single-batch correction framework with two components: 1) Cross-fitted preconditioning, which splits the training batch into independent microbatch groups to estimate gradient and preconditioner separately to eliminate coupling bias; 2) Variance-corrected inversion, which uses cross-microbatch variance to cancel the first-order bias introduced by preconditioner inversion. The framework is adapted to three representative optimizer families covering diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning.","experiments":"Evaluated on pretraining and instruction tuning tasks, the corrected AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo achieve 0.1489, 0.0701, and 0.1103 nat reductions in held-out pretraining cross-entropy respectively, with consistent gains across all tested optimizer families and the largest improvement observed on AdamW.","reading_guide":"For a high-level overview, read Chapter 2 (problem setup) and Chapter 3 (bias decomposition), which takes around 10 minutes. Effective adaptive optimizers exhibit an implicit $L_{\\infty}$ bias favoring stability, yet, sparsity requires an $L_1$ bias. To integrate sparsity, we propose a composition of optimizer steps, which we cast as non-commutative operators to analyze and combine their optimization geometry in a principled way. This yields HORST (Hyperbolic Opera","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T12:34:58+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21104.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer 稀疏训练常遇到一个优化层面的冲突：AdamW 等自适应优化器带来稳定训练，但其更新几何更接近 L∞ 偏置，倾向于让权重幅度更均匀；高稀疏度需要的是 L1 偏置，倾向于把权重质量集中到少量参数上。HORST 试图在不放弃自适应优化稳定性的前提下，引入有利于稀疏解的优化几何。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练研究的价值在于把“Transformer 为什么难以高稀疏训练”从剪枝策略问题推进到优化器隐式偏置问题。它不是单纯加一个稀疏正则，也不是训练后剪枝，而是讨论训练轨迹如何塑造可稀疏化的权重分布。对大模型预训练来说，结论有两个启发。第一，如果目标是从训练早期就得到可稀疏模型，AdamW 的稳定性和稀疏性可能天然有张力。第二，优化器可以通过组合不同几何来设计，而不是只在 AdamW 的学习率、weight decay、momentum 上调参。工程侧仍需谨慎：非结构化稀疏是否能带来真实吞吐收益，取决于硬件和 kernel；论文的语言模型规模如果不大，不能直接外推到大规模 LLM 预训练。","technical_approach":"论文把优化步骤看作非交换的函数算子，而不是简单的参数更新公式。这个视角用于解释为什么步骤顺序会影响最终隐式偏置。自适应优化器近似 sign descent，属于 steepest descent 几何，优势是训练稳定，但对应 L∞ 偏置；直接追求 L1 steepest descent 会退化到 coordinate descent，每步只更新最大梯度坐标，在大网络中不可用。另一条路线是 mirror descent。论文使用 hyperbolic entropy mirror map 来引入 L1 偏置，因为它可以鼓励权重质量集中，从而有利于稀疏。HORST 的设计是把标准自适应优化步骤和 hyperbolic mirror step 按特定顺序组合，让模型先获得 AdamW 类方法的稳定下降，再由 mirror map 改写一部分隐式偏置。Theorem 4.7 给出理论动机：entropy mirror map 可以覆盖 steepest-descent 的隐式偏置，这解释了为什么简单相加或随意交替不够，组合顺序是方法的一部分。","experiments":"实验覆盖视觉 Transformer 和语言任务中的 sparse training。根据给定内容，论文报告 HORST 相比 AdamW baseline 在不同稀疏度下有稳定提升，并且高稀疏区间收益最大，这与它针对 L1 稀疏偏置的设计一致。给定正文片段没有提供可核验的具体数值、模型规模、token 数或语言模型 perplexity 变化，因此不宜补写数字。阅读实验时应重点确认三点：是否是训练中稀疏而非训练后剪枝；稀疏度提高时性能退化曲线是否变缓；优化器额外开销是否会抵消稀疏带来的潜在收益。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。先读 Introduction 和 Figure 1，理解 AdamW 训练的 Transformer 权重分布为什么不利于稀疏。Figure 2 是方法动机的核心：steepest descent 和 mirror descent 分别适合不同隐式偏置。第 4 节的 operator composition 是最值得看的技术部分，尤其是非交换组合和 Theorem 4.7。实验部分建议只抓高稀疏区间的曲线和语言任务设置，判断它是否接近 LLM sparse pretraining 的真实约束。如果只关心大模型预训练，可跳过视觉任务细节，把重点放在优化器几何、额外计算开销和稀疏模式是否硬件友好。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Sparse Transformer training faces an optimization conflict. Adaptive optimizers such as AdamW are stable and are therefore standard for Transformers, but their update geometry is associated with an L-infinity-like implicit bias that tends to equalize weight magnitudes. High sparsity needs an L1-like bias, where weight mass concentrates on a small subset of parameters. HORST tries to combine these two requirements: stable adaptive training and a sparsity-inducing optimization geometry.","importance":"The useful contribution is the framing of Transformer sparsification as an optimizer-geometry problem, not only as a pruning or regularization problem. For pretraining, this matters because the training trajectory determines whether a model becomes easy or hard to sparsify. The paper suggests that AdamW may be inherently misaligned with high-sparsity solutions, even when it is the right choice for stable training. It also gives a design pattern for optimizers: compose update operators with different geometries instead of only tuning AdamW hyperparameters. The practical impact depends on scale and hardware. Non-structured sparsity does not automatically translate into throughput gains, and results from smaller language settings should not be directly extrapolated to large LLM pretraining.","technical_approach":"The paper treats optimizer steps as non-commutative functional operators. This is important because the order of composition changes the implicit bias. Adaptive methods are linked to sign descent and steepest-descent geometry, which provides stable bounded updates but induces an L-infinity bias. Direct L1 steepest descent corresponds to coordinate descent, which is not practical for large neural networks. The alternative is mirror descent. HORST uses a hyperbolic entropy mirror map to introduce an L1 sparsity bias while retaining an adaptive optimizer step for stability. The method composes a standard adaptive step with a hyperbolic mirror step in a specific order. Theorem 4.7 provides the key motivation: the entropy mirror map can overwrite the implicit bias of the steepest-descent step, so the ordering is not an implementation detail but part of the method.","experiments":"The experiments cover sparse training for vision Transformers and language tasks. From the provided content, HORST consistently improves over AdamW baselines across sparsity levels, with the largest gains in the high-sparsity regime where AdamW struggles most. The provided text does not include verifiable numerical results, model sizes, token counts, or language-model perplexity deltas, so specific numbers should be checked in Section 6. The most important experimental questions are whether the sparsity is induced during training rather than after-the-fact pruning, whether the degradation curve becomes flatter as sparsity increases, and whether the optimizer overhead is small enough to matter in real sparse-training pipelines.","reading_guide":"A focused skim takes about 20–30 minutes. 先执行三次 L 更新，再执行一次 H 更新；最后用 H state 接线性头产生 token logits。这样做的动机是用参数复用换取更深的计算过程，而不是简单堆更多 Transformer block。\n\n循环结构用于语言建模的主要风险是梯度和激活不稳定。作者引入 MagicNorm，并配合 warmup deep credit assignment。后者训练初期只把梯度回传到最后两个 追平。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether competitive language-model training from scratch is possible without trillion-token corpora and large compute clusters. Instead of following the standard recipe of a larger Transformer trained on raw web text, HRM-Text changes both the architecture and the objective: it uses a hierarchical recurrent model to obtain deeper effective computation, and it trains directly on instruction-response pairs with a task-completion loss.","importance":"The main contribution is a concrete low-budget pretraining recipe that puts sample efficiency back into architecture and objective design. The result suggests that the compute-to-performance curve can change substantially when the model has a useful structural prior and the training distribution is aligned with conditional generation. It also blurs the usual pretrain/posttrain boundary: the model is trained from scratch on instruction-response data, with loss only on the answer. memory）”扩到很大规模，同时不把训练成本也按参数量一起放大。以 Engram 为代表的 n-gram 哈希记忆在推理侧很省（O(1) 查表、吞吐损失小），但训练侧需要从零学习巨大的记忆表，记忆参数占比过高时训练效率会明显下降。论文要解决的是：能不能把记忆表从“要训练的参数”变成“可复用的知识载体”，把主要成本挪到一次性的离线计算。","importance":"这是少见的、直接面向 pretrain scaling 的结构性改动：用离线构建的外部记忆把容量扩展从“训练时算力/可训练参数”中部分剥离出来。它的价值不在又做了一个 retrieval，而在提出一种可操作的范式：用一个已训练好的 grafting model 生成并冻结记忆值，让 recipient 在预训练时以很小的额外可训练开销获得“局部模式（local n-gram）”的表示能力。对数据效率/训练效率的潜在影响点包括：\n- 让模型把频繁、表面形式决定语义的片段（实体、公式化短语、代码调用、数学记号）更多交给外部记忆，减少主干参数反复拟合。\n- 通过 exact longest-match suffix lookup 提高“命中时的语义确定性”，减少哈希冲突带来的噪声。\n- 把“扩容量”从 MoE 的稀疏计算路线，补充为“稀疏存储 + 表示迁移”的路线，适合在训练预算固定时做容量外扩。","technical_approach":"Memory Grafting 在 Engram 框架上做了三处关键改造：\n1）记忆值的来源：不再从零训练一个巨大的哈希表，而是离线枚举/收集高频局部 n-gram（论文表述为 frequent local n-grams），用一个 grafting model 跑一遍，把这些 n-gram 在“最后一个 token 位置”的最终层 hidden state 存成 memory value，并冻结。直觉是：成熟模型对这些固定短语已经学到稳定表征，直接拿来当记忆比从零学更省。\n2）寻址方式：对这些高频 n-gram 走“精确匹配 + 最长后缀（longest-match suffix）”查找，而不是纯哈希。这样命中时是确定的 n-gram→value 映射，覆盖会随表变大而单调提升。\n3）融合与兜底：recipient 在每个 token 位置先尝试 exact lookup，命中则取出冻结 value，经轻量 projection + gate 以残差方式注入到隐藏状态；未命中则回退到原始 Engram 的 hash-based memory（可训练）以保证覆盖。也就是说：高频模式用“冻结的、来自 grafting model 的确定性记忆”，长尾模式用“可训练的哈希记忆”。\n从优化角度看，这相当于把一部分表示学习变成了“离线蒸馏到表”，训练时只学如何使用它（gate/projection）以及补齐长尾（fallback memory）。","experiments":"论文宣称在“匹配的 recipient 架构与相同预训练预算”下，Memory Grafting 相比 MoE 与 vanilla Engram 有增益，并展示：\n- 随着 exact-memory table 变大，exact 覆盖率提升；\n- 推理效率可用（延续 Engram 的 O(1) 查表与较小吞吐损失的叙述）；\n- probing 证据表明 recipient 确实在用 grafted memory（而不是被主干忽略）。\n当前给出的全文片段未包含关键表格数字（如 backbone 参数量、memory 参数量/表大小、训练 token 数、具体基准提升幅度、与 MoE/Engram 的对齐设置、以及“70% memory 参数占比后训练效率下降”的复现实验曲线）。精读时需要重点核对：1）在相同训练 FLOPs 下的 loss/perplexity 曲线；2）不同 table size 的收益是否呈现可预期的 scaling；3）fallback memory 的训练量是否成为新的瓶颈。","reading_guide":"精读建议（建议 1.5–2 小时，值得逐段看公式与实现细节）：\n- Introduction 里关于 Engram 训练效率随 memory 占比下降的经验结论，是动机的关键；需要确认他们的复现实验设置。\n- Method 重点看：频繁 n-gram 集合如何构建（阈值、n 的范围、是否做 vocab compression P）、离线跑 grafting model 取哪一层/哪种 hidden state、exact longest-match 的实现与复杂度、以及 gate/projection 的注入位置（残差分支 c 的定义）。\n- 关注“hash-based Engram fallback”如何与 exact table 共同工作：命中优先级、是否混合多阶 n、以及训练时梯度只流向哪些部分。\n- Experiments 重点找三类证据：\n  1) 与 MoE、vanilla Engram 在相同预算下的对比（不仅是最终指标，也要看收敛速度/稳定性）。\n  2) table size 扩展带来的覆盖率与性能曲线（是否出现饱和、是否对不同域数据敏感）。\n  3) probing/ablation：去掉 gate、去掉 exact lookup、只留 fallback、换 grafting model 强弱等，来证明“确实在用 grafted 表示”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to scale conditional memory for language-model pretraining without scaling training cost proportionally. Hash-based n-gram memories (e.g., Engram) are inference-efficient (O(1) lookup, small throughput hit) but expensive to train because the large memory table is learned from scratch; once memory dominates parameter count, training efficiency degrades. The paper asks: can we turn a big chunk of memory from trainable parameters into reusable, offline-built knowledge?","importance":"A concrete pretraining-scaling idea: decouple capacity expansion from training-time optimization by building a large external memory offline and training only lightweight adapters plus a long-tail fallback. The contribution is less about retrieval per se and more about a workable recipe for representation-level transfer into a conditional-memory table. It suggests a complementary scaling axis to MoE: sparse storage + offline representation grafting, potentially improving training efficiency on frequent, surface-form-determined patterns (entities, formulaic phrases, code calls, math notation).","technical_approach":"Memory Grafting modifies Engram-style conditional memory in three key ways:\n1) Memory values are not learned from scratch. Instead, collect frequent local n-gram keys, run a pretrained “grafting model” offline on those n-grams, and store the final-token hidden states as frozen memory values.\n2) Addressing uses exact longest-match suffix lookup for those frequent n-grams, giving deterministic hits and coverage that increases with table size (as opposed to pure hashing with collisions).\n3) Fusion keeps Engram’s gated residual injection but applies lightweight projections/gates to adapt the frozen retrieved vectors to the recipient model. For contexts not covered by the exact table, it falls back to a hash-based Engram memory (trainable) to preserve coverage. Net effect: frequent patterns are served by frozen, high-quality representations; the trainable component focuses on using them (gates/projections) and covering the tail (fallback memory).","experiments":"Claims under matched recipient architectures and pretraining budgets: improvements over MoE and vanilla Engram; exact-memory coverage increases as the exact table grows; inference remains practical; probing indicates the recipient actually uses the grafted memory. 的正交相对位置结构，导致距离感和时空关系被削弱。","importance":"这篇论文与 LLM 预训练的关系在于架构和长序列建模。它不是文本 LM 训练 recipe，但它讨论了 3D RoPE、稀疏-低秩分解、长序列注意力近似这些基础问题。对多模态基座模型有两点启发：第一，视频 token 的 attention 结构比文本更容易出现空间局部性和低频背景场，可以用更强的结构假设换效率；第二，RoPE 不是一个可以随意丢进线性 attention 的位置编码，近似 attention 时必须考虑相对位置结构是否被保留。","technical_approach":"RoPeSLR 提出 3D RoPE-guided Sparse-LowRank attention。论文给出一个存在性结果：在经验可验证的假设下，DiT 的 post-softmax attention matrix 可以分解为稀疏高频语义 spike 集合和低秩背景连续项。借助 3D RoPE 的 Fourier 展开，作者声称高频 spike 规模为 O(L^{3/2})，低秩背景的秩需求为 O(d_h log L)。实现上，它把计算责任分开：block-sparse attention，例如 VMoBA，负责主要加速和局部/关键依赖；非线性 low-rank MLP compensator 负责恢复全局低频上下文。这个 compensator 不走标准 positive feature map 的线性 attention 路线，而是用 token-wise amortized MLP 近似低秩背景场，并注入可学习 3D absolute positional embedding，以弥补线性聚合破坏 3D RoPE 相对距离结构的问题。最后用 distribution-aware gated fusion 融合稀疏分支和低秩补偿分支。","experiments":"实验覆盖大规模视频 DiT，包括 Wan2.1-1.3B、Wan2.1-14B 和 Hunyuan13B。论文报告在超过 100K token 的超长视频上，RoPeSLR 可达到最高 Diffusion Transformers. Video DiTs operate on long spatiotemporal token sequences, so full attention scales as O(L²). Sparse attention reduces cost but loses global context when sparsity becomes aggressive. Sparse-plus-linear hybrids recover some global information, but standard linear attention does not preserve the orthogonal relative-position structure of 3D RoPE, weakening distance awareness and spatiotemporal alignment.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model research is architectural rather than pretraining-recipe oriented. flow in Diffusion Transformers. The target is not the attention module, tokenization, or diffusion objective, but the inherited PreNorm residual stream. Standard residual addition creates three coupled issues in DiTs: monotonic hidden-state magnitude growth, sharp backward gradient decay, and increasing block-level redundancy.","importance":"This is an architecture and training-dynamics paper with useful signals for foundation-model research. It revisits a residual-stream issue known from LLMs, but in DiTs the denoising timestep adds an extra axis: high-noise steps emphasize coarse structure, while low-noise steps require fine detail. A fixed, timestep-agnostic residual accumulation rule is therefore a questionable default. The lesson also matters for unified multimodal pretraining, where design choices inherited from autoregressive Transformers may not transfer cleanly to diffusion, flow, or masked generative models.","technical_approach":"The paper first diagnoses DiTs along both depth and denoising timestep, measuring hidden-state magnitude, gradient flow, and block-wise redundancy. It then proposes Diffusion-Adaptive Routing, or DAR, as a drop-in replacement for ordinary residual accumulation. Instead of passing a running residual through the stack, each sublayer aggregates previous sublayer outputs through a softmax attention over depth. The query is computed from the current adaLN-modulated hidden state, which gives the routing both content dependence and timestep dependence. This differs from U-Net-style long skip connections: it does not manually pair shallow and deep layers, and it does not impose a fixed hierarchy. It keeps the Transformer stack homogeneous while making cross-layer aggregation learnable, non-incremental, and timestep-adaptive. The method is also compatible with representation-alignment objectives such as REPA.","experiments":"The main experiments are on ImageNet 256×256. With SiT-XL/2, DAR reaches 7.56 FID, improving the matched-compute vanilla SiT baseline by 2.11 FID. It also reaches the baseline’s converged quality with roughly 8.75× fewer training iterations. When combined with REPA, DAR gives about a 2× early-stage training acceleration over REPA alone. The paper further links the quality gains to the diagnostic measurements: DAR reduces the magnitude inflation, gradient decay, and block redundancy observed under the standard residual stream.","reading_guide":"Skim to semi-deep read in about 30–45 minutes. In the experiments, focus on the FID-versus-iteration trade-off, the combination with REPA, and the connection between diagnostic metrics and generation quality. A useful follow-up question is whether similar routing can help diffusion LMs or masked language models, and what memory overhead the depth attention introduces.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"DiT残差信息流的分析对理解扩散模型训练动态有启发，DAR即插即用的设计值得在LLM扩散建模范式中尝试复现。只看§3诊断分析和§4 DAR设计即可。","en":"The analysis of residual information flow in DiTs sheds light on diffusion model training dynamics; DAR's drop-in design warrants reproduction attempts in diffusion LLM paradigms. WONN e","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T09:08:00+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20922.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文试图回答一个架构问题：同步振荡动力学能否从小规模、专用的 object discovery 场景扩展到标准视觉识别和组合推理任务，并成为一种参数效率较高的神经网络计算方式。","importance":"这篇论文和 LLM 预训练的关系是间接的，但对架构研究有参考价值。它提供了一种不同于 Transformer、CNN、SSM 的表示演化方式：状态不是普通实值向量的静态层间映射，而是在环面相位空间上迭代同步。对基座模型研究者来说，值得看的点是它如何把连续动力系统、迭代推理和参数效率结合起来，尤其是 maze、Sudoku 这类需要多步约束传播的任务。","technical_approach":"WONN 基于 generalized Winfree dynamics。每个神经元被表示为相位振荡器，整体状态位于高维环面 (S1)^d 上。和 Kuramoto 模型主要依赖成对相位差不同，Winfree 形式把交互拆成 sensitivity function 和 influence function，因此可以表达更丰富的同步结构。论文提供两类交互实现：固定三角函数映射，以及可学习神经网络映射。它还引入 grouped dynamics，用分组方式支持局部协调和全局信息流。另一个设计是 phase-frequency 双状态：相位用于较快的同步更新，频率用于较慢的表征演化，从而支持稳定的迭代 refinement。对三角交互形式，作者还给出 interaction energy，用作稳定性视角和推理任务诊断信号。","experiments":"实验包括 CIFAR、ImageNet-1K、Maze-hard 和 Sudoku。作者报告 WONN 在视觉任务上能达到与卷积网络、Transformer 类模型和已有同步架构相近或更好的准确率，同时参数量更低。ImageNet-1K 结果用于证明振荡同步架构可以扩展到标准大规模视觉 benchmark。推理任务上，WONN 在 Maze-hard 上用约为 HRM 1% 的参数达到有竞争力的表现；在 Sudoku 上达到满分测试准确率。论文还观察到 learned representation 中出现双峰相位组织，不同同步相位组分别偏向粗粒度和细粒度图像结构。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30 分钟。优先看 Section 2 的动力学定义，特别是 Winfree 与 Kuramoto 的差异、sensitivity / influence 分解、grouped dynamics 和 phase-frequency 双状态。视觉实验可快速看参数量与准确率表，不必细抠每个 baseline。推理实验更值得停留，尤其是 Maze-hard、Sudoku 和 interaction energy 的诊断图。若从 LLM 架构角度阅读，重点不是它的视觉结果，而是迭代相位同步是否能作为一种轻量的约束传播模块。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether synchronization-based oscillatory dynamics can move beyond small, specialized object-discovery settings and scale to standard vision benchmarks and combinatorial reasoning tasks.","importance":"The connection to LLM pretraining is indirect, but the architectural signal is useful. WONN represents a different computation pattern from Transformers, CNNs, and SSMs: representations evolve through iterative synchronization on a toroidal phase space rather than through a purely feed-forward stack of real-valued transformations. For foundation-model researchers, the interesting part is the link between continuous dynamics, iterative refinement, and parameter efficiency, especially on tasks that require constraint propagation such as mazes and Sudoku.","technical_approach":"WONN is built on generalized Winfree dynamics. Each neuron is a phase oscillator, and the full representation lies on a high-dimensional torus, (S1)^d. Unlike Kuramoto-style systems, which mainly depend on pairwise phase differences, Winfree dynamics separate interactions into sensitivity and influence functions. This gives the model more flexible synchronization behavior. The interaction functions can be fixed trigonometric maps or learnable neural networks. The architecture also uses grouped dynamics to combine local coordination with global information flow. A phase-frequency state design separates fast phase synchronization from slower frequency evolution, which supports stable iterative refinement. For trigonometric interactions, the model admits an interaction energy that can be used as a stability diagnostic, especially in reasoning tasks.","experiments":"The experiments cover CIFAR, ImageNet-1K, Maze-hard, and Sudoku. The paper reports that WONN matches or exceeds convolutional, Transformer-like, and prior synchronization-inspired baselines while using fewer parameters. The ImageNet-1K result is used to show that oscillatory synchronization can scale to a standard large vision benchmark. On reasoning tasks, WONN reaches competitive Maze-hard performance with about 1% of the parameters of HRM, and achieves perfect test accuracy on Sudoku. The representation analysis reports bimodal phase organization, with different synchronized phase groups specializing in coarse and fine image structure.","reading_guide":"A focused skim takes about 30 minutes. Start with the dynamics in Section 2: the difference between Winfree and Kuramoto dynamics, the sensitivity/influence decomposition, grouped dynamics, and the phase-frequency state. For the vision results, read the accuracy-versus-parameter tables rather than every baseline detail. Spend more time on the reasoning experiments, especially Maze-hard, Sudoku, and the interaction-energy diagnostics. From an LLM-architecture perspective, the useful question is whether iterative phase synchronization can serve as a lightweight constraint-propagation module, not whether the model is a direct replacement for Transformer pretraining.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"振荡神经网络架构的系统性探索，相位编码和环面表示的思路对思考非Transformer建模范式有启发性，重点看§3的动力学设计。","en":"Systematic exploration of oscillatory neural network architectures; phase encoding and torus representation ideas are provocative for non-Transformer paradigm thinking; focus on §3 dynamics design."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Shanghai Qi Zhi Institute","Tsinghua University"],"authors":["Jiawen Dai","Yue Song"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["neural-architecture","oscillatory-networks","dynamical-systems","winfree-dynamics","representation-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20922","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.20922","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20187","title":"Neural Estimation of Pairwise Mutual Information in Masked Discrete Sequence Models","abstract":"Understanding dependencies between variables is critical for interpretability and efficient generation in masked diffusion models (MDMs), yet these models primarily expose marginal conditional distributions and do not explicitly represent inter-variable dependence. We propose a neural framework for estimating pairwise conditional mutual information (MI) directly from the hidden states of a pretrained MDM, using ground-truth MI computed from the model's own conditional distributions for supervisi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-01-27T22:30:16+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20187.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.IT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注 masked discrete sequence model，尤其是 masked diffusion model 里一个长期存在但很少被显式建模的问题：模型会输出每个位置的边缘条件分布，但不会直接告诉你“两个位置在当前上下文下到底有多依赖”。没有这个依赖结构，解释模型内部决策很难，做并行解码也容易把本该互相条件化的位置一起采样，导致全局一致性变差。作者要解决的是：能否直接从预训练 MDM 的隐藏状态里估计 pairwise conditional mutual information，并把它用于解释和更稳的并行采样。","importance":"对主流文本预训练的直接影响有限，但对非 AR 建模范式，尤其 masked/any-order/diffusion LM，很有启发。它的价值不在于提出一个新的生成目标，而在于补上了“模型内部依赖结构不可见”这一层。对于任何想把 masked discrete model 推向更高效采样的人，这个问题都绕不过去。相比只看 token entropy 的动态解码策略，MI 给出的不是“哪个位置不确定”，而是“哪些位置不能一起决定”。这对并行生成的正确性比置信度更贴近问题本身。另一个价值是 interpretability：如果一个 MDM 学到的依赖图能被稳定读出来，就能更细地分析模型在结构化任务上的归纳偏置。","technical_approach":"方法核心是训练一个神经估计器，从预训练 MDM 的隐藏状态直接预测任意位置对在给定上下文下的条件互信息 I(X_i; X_j | C)。关键点有两个：监督信号来自模型自身，而不是外部标注；预测目标是整张 pairwise MI matrix，而不是逐对单独跑昂贵估计。\n\n论文的出发点是，标准 MDM 训练只学 p(x_i | x_t) 这类边缘条件分布。若要知道两个位置是否可并行采样，需要 joint dependence 信息。作者利用模型自身可访问的条件分布构造“ground-truth MI”作为监督，再训练一个额外网络从 hidden states 回归这些 MI 值。这样做避免了传统 MI 估计里高维密度估计的困难，也避免在推理时为每一对位置显式枚举联合分布。\n\n从文中描述看，估计器输入是当前 masked sequence 在 backbone 中产生的 position-wise hidden states，输出是全序列的 pairwise MI 预测。也就是说，它试图在一次前向里恢复一个 L×L 的依赖图。这个设计比逐 pair 估计更适合推理时使用，因为复杂度主要落在一次矩阵预测，而不是 O(L^2) 次独立查询。\n\n应用层面，作者把 MI 用在两件事上。第一是解释：在 Sudoku 和蛋白序列上观察哪些位置被模型视为强依赖。第二是 MI-guided parallel decoding：优先同时采样条件近似独立的一组变量，避免像 confidence-based unmasking 那样只按边缘熵挑“看起来容易”的位置。论文明确把自己和 Mask-Predict、基于 entropy 的动态解码、以及用边缘熵上界依赖误差的做法区分开来。它的主张是，熵只能反映单点不确定性，不能显式表示 pairwise conditional dependence，因此在强结构约束任务上会误判可并行性。","experiments":"实验主要在两个场景：Sudoku 和 protein sequences。前者是强约束、强结构依赖的离散推理任务；后者代表长链离散序列生成，且并行采样有实际效率意义。论文的实验目标不是刷通用生成指标，而是验证两件事：MI 估计器能否从隐藏状态恢复模型内部依赖结构；以及这种依赖图能否指导更合理的并行解码。\n\n从全文可见的结果方向看，作者展示了估计器能够较好预测 pairwise MI matrix，并在可视化上恢复出符合任务结构的依赖模式。Sudoku 中，相关格子之间的依赖更强；蛋白序列中，某些功能或结构相关位置会呈现更高的条件依赖。更重要的是，MI-guided decoding 相比仅按 token confidence 或 entropy 的策略，在并行采样时更能维持一致性，减少把强耦合变量同时解开的错误。\n\n不过，这篇工作的实验规模和结论边界也要看清。它更像一个机制与方法验证，而不是大规模 foundation model 结论。摘要提到使用 Sudoku 和 ESM-C 蛋白序列，但在给出的全文片段里没有看到足够完整的具体数字、模型规模、速度提升倍数或误差下降幅度，因此不能把它解读成已经证明了在大规模文本 diffusion LM 上的通用收益。更稳妥的理解是：它提供了一个可操作的依赖估计框架，并在两个结构化离散域上证明了可行性。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries，确认作者到底在估计什么量：不是普通 MI，而是给定当前上下文的 pairwise conditional MI。然后看方法部分里 ground-truth MI 如何从 pretrained MDM 的条件分布构造出来，以及估计器如何从 hidden states 一次性预测整张 MI matrix。这是全文最值得看的地方。\n\n实验部分优先看两类图：一类是 MI 预测与真实 MI 的对齐结果，判断估计器是否真的读出了模型内部依赖；另一类是 MI-guided parallel decoding 与 entropy/confidence-based decoding 的对比，判断它是否带来实际采样收益。Sudoku 的可视化通常最直观，蛋白实验则更接近作者想强调的长序列生成场景。预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主要思路；如果你在做 diffusion LM 或 any-order LM，建议再细看复杂度讨论和失败案例，判断这种方法能否扩展到更长文本序列。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a missing piece in masked discrete sequence models, especially masked diffusion models: the model exposes marginal conditional distributions for each position, but it does not explicitly expose how strongly two positions depend on each other under the current context. Without that dependency structure, interpretation is limited and parallel decoding can easily sample strongly coupled variables at the same time, hurting global consistency. The goal is to estimate pairwise conditional mutual information directly from the hidden states of a pretrained MDM and use it for both interpretability and dependency-aware parallel generation.","importance":"Its direct impact on mainstream text pretraining is limited, but it is relevant for alternative language-modeling paradigms such as masked, any-order, and diffusion-based discrete generation. The main contribution is not a new training objective for the base model. It is a way to recover the model’s internal dependency graph. That matters because many accelerated decoding strategies rely only on token-level confidence or entropy, which tells you where uncertainty is high but not which variables should not be sampled together. Mutual information is a more appropriate signal for parallelization because it targets conditional dependence rather than marginal uncertainty. The interpretability angle is also useful: if dependency structure can be read out reliably from hidden states, one can study what structural biases the model has learned.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对 diffusion/MDLM 研究员值得看方法部分；实验不是文本 LLM，但 MI-guided decoding 是可迁移的好想法。","en":"Worth reading for diffusion/MDLM researchers; the experiments are not text LMs, but MI-guided decoding is a transferable idea."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jai Sharma","Yifan Wang","Bryan Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["masked-diffusion","discrete-sequence-modeling","mutual-information","parallel-decoding","protein-modeling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20187","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.20187","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20670","title":"LT2: Linear-Time Looped Transformers","abstract":"Looped Transformers (LT) have emerged as a powerful architecture by iterating their layers multiple times before decoding the final token. However, pairing them with full attention retains quadratic complexity, making them computationally expensive and slow. We introduce LT2 (Linear-Time Looped Transformers), a family of looped architectures that replace quadratic softmax attention with subquadratic, linear-time attention. We study two variants: LT2-linear with linear attention and LT2-sparse wi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T03:36:44+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20670.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Looped Transformer 用权重共享把“深度”换成多轮 latent computation，但每一轮仍要做全注意力。这样参数量省下来了，训练 FLOPs、长上下文推理延迟和 KV-cache 却随 loop 次数继续放大。LT2 解决的是这个不匹配：如何让 looped computation 继续保留多轮推理收益，同时把 token mixing Transformer 的权重共享框架，把每个共享 block 中的 full MHA 替换为线性注意力或稀疏注意力。论文研究了 LT2-linear 和 LT2-sparse 两类。LT2-linear 使用线性注意力类 mixer，通过 recurrent state 在 loop 间被反复更新；loop 不只是重复同一个近似注意力，而是在多轮中细化记忆状态。LT2-sparse for multiple iterations, turning repeated computation into effective depth. The problem is that each iteration still runs full self-attention, so the attention cost and KV-cache footprint grow poorly with sequence length and with the number of loops. LT2 addresses this mismatch: it tries to keep the benefits of looped latent computation while replacing quadratic token mixing with linear or sparse alternatives.","importance":"The paper is relevant as an architecture and training-efficiency study rather than a new pretraining objective. It makes looped computation more plausible for long-context and parameter-efficient language models by attacking the attention bottleneck directly. The useful idea is that looping is not just depth reuse: with linear attention it repeatedly refines a recurrent memory state, and with sparse attention it expands the effective receptive field over iterations. The main caveat is scale. The reported results are encouraging, but the behavior at larger model sizes, longer pretraining runs, and more diverse data mixtures is still an open question.","technical_approach":"LT2 keeps the standard Looped Transformer structure: a set of shared blocks is applied for T iterations before prediction. The change is inside the token mixer. LT2-linear replaces full MHA with a linear-attention primitive, so the recurrent attention state can be updated and refined across loop iterations. LT2-sparse replaces full attention with sparse attention, where repeated application gradually expands the reachable context. The paper also studies hybrid designs. The GDN + DSA hybrid combines linear and sparse attention without any quadratic full-attention layer. The Full + GDN hybrid keeps a small amount of full attention and uses linear attention for the rest, trading a little quadratic cost for better quality. A zero-initialized per-channel learned gate is used to make the replacement more stable.","experiments":"The main results cover zero-shot evaluation, language modeling, recall, state tracking, and inference efficiency. The GDN + DSA LT2-hybrid matches the standard full-attention Looped Transformer at 59.3% average zero-shot accuracy while reaching about 125 tokens/s at 8k context and batch size 8, compared with 22 tokens/s for the standard looped model, roughly a 5.7x decode-throughput gain. The Full + GDN hybrid improves the average zero-shot score from 59.3% to 61.4%, a 2.1-point gain, while still giving around a 5x throughput improvement in the same setting. The paper also converts a pretrained Ouro looped model into an LT2-style hybrid model with about 1B tokens of continued training. The resulting Ouro-Hybrid-1.4B largely retains the full-attention teacher quality while gaining the LT2 efficiency profile.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25–35 minutes. This provides a simple and flexible way to inject external constraints (e.g., cost functions or pre-trained verifiers) for controlled generation. However, existing methods often fail when composing multiple constraints simultaneously, which leads to deviations from the true data manifold. In this work, we identify root causes of","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T05:56:55+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20758.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CV","cs.LG","cs.RO"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是 flow / diffusion 模型在推理时同时满足多个 reward 或约束时的偏移问题。普通 additive guidance 会把多个 reward 梯度直接相加；当这些梯度方向冲突时，采样轨迹容易离开训练数据流形，导致 pretrained vector field 在低密度区域失准。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在生成模型的“预训练先验 + 推理时控制”接口。它说明 reward composition 不是简单加权问题，梯度几何会直接影响样本是否还处在模型学到的分布附近。这个结论对 diffusion LM、flow matching LM、跨模态生成模型都有参考意义：如果后训练或推理时控制信号互相冲突，只优化 reward 可能会破坏预训练先验。","technical_approach":"论文先分析 approximate guidance 的误差来源，把 guided sampling 的近似误差分成 coupling shift、gradient misalignment 和 localized approximation error 三项。关键观察是，在多个 reward 的组合场景里，误差会随 reward 数量 G 和梯度平均夹角项 1 - cos phi 增大而快速放大。基于这个分析，作者提出 Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance，即 CAR guidance。它不是改动底层 flow 模型，也不是使用代价更高的 exact guidance，而是在推理时检测 reward 梯度之间的冲突，并用一个 conflict-aware gating 机制只在冲突明显的区域激活可学习修正项。这样保留 additive guidance 的轻量和可插拔特点，同时减少 off-manifold drift。","experiments":"实验覆盖 2D 合成分布、像素空间图像编辑、状态空间规划和 3D 点云机器人操作。结果集中验证一个点：当 reward 数量增加或目标互相牵制时，普通 guidance 容易提高约束满足率但损害样本保真度，CAR guidance 在两者之间更稳。论文报告图像编辑中 identity preservation 提升 25.4%；规划任务 success rate 提升 38.75%；机器人操作里 violation rate 降低 78%，success 从 9% 提升到 61%。这些数字说明方法主要收益来自冲突场景，而不是单一约束下的常规 guidance 调参。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25 到 35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 off-manifold drift 的问题设定，Section 4 的误差分解和梯度错配项，这是最有信息量的部分。方法部分关注 gating 何时启动、修正项如何学习、是否依赖 reward 类型。实验部分优先看 compositional reward 的消融：reward 数量、梯度夹角、样本保真度和约束满足率之间的关系。若关心 LLM 或 diffusion LM，可重点借鉴它对“多个训练/推理信号冲突”的诊断方式，而不是具体机器人任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies inference-time guidance for flow and diffusion-style generative models under multiple simultaneous rewards. Standard additive guidance simply sums reward gradients. When these gradients disagree, the sampling trajectory can be pushed away from the data manifold, where the pretrained vector field is poorly calibrated.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model pretraining is the interface between a pretrained generative prior and runtime control signals. The paper makes a concrete point: composing rewards is not only a matter of choosing weights. The geometry of reward gradients determines whether guidance preserves the learned distribution. This is useful for diffusion LMs, flow-matching LMs, and multimodal generative models where post-training or inference-time objectives may conflict with the pretrained prior.","technical_approach":"The paper first derives an error decomposition for approximate guided sampling. The approximation error is separated into coupling shift, gradient misalignment, and localized approximation error. The key term is gradient misalignment: in compositional reward settings, the error grows with both the number of rewards G and an angular disagreement factor, 1 - cos phi. This motivates Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance, or CAR guidance. CAR keeps the lightweight nature of additive inference-time guidance, but adds a conflict-aware gating mechanism. The gate detects regions with strong reward-gradient disagreement and selectively activates a learnable correction term. This avoids the cost and rigidity of exact guidance while reducing off-manifold drift.","experiments":"The evaluation spans 2D synthetic benchmarks, pixel-space image editing, state-based planning, and 3D point-cloud robotic manipulation. The reported gains are largest in settings with genuinely competing constraints. In image editing, identity preservation improves by 25.4%. In planning, success rates increase by 38.75%. In robotic manipulation, violation rates are reduced by 78%, and success rises from 9% to 61%. The experiments support the paper’s central claim: naive guidance can improve reward satisfaction at the cost of sample fidelity, while conflict-aware correction better preserves the pretrained generative prior.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. Start with the problem setup in the introduction, then focus on the theoretical section that decomposes guidance error and identifies the gradient-misalignment term. In the method section, check when the gate is activated, how the correction is learned, and whether the mechanism assumes a specific reward family. In the experiments, prioritize ablations over the number of rewards, gradient conflict, constraint satisfaction, and sample fidelity. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T22:49:05+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20547.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有流匹配和扩散生成模型常借助辅助随机过程（扩展状态空间上的马尔可夫过程）来训练，但生成时仅需在观测空间上模拟，需要将扩展状态的生成器投影到观测空间。目前这类投影结果均为案例式推导，且限制辅助状态为离散型，缺乏统一理论框架。","importance":"为扩散/流匹配建模范式提供了更通用的理论基础，允许设计新的训练方案——例如用连续或流形值的辅助过程而非仅离散辅助变量。这对设计更灵活的 token 级生成（如变长文本、视频帧插入）有理论指导意义。","technical_approach":"提出隐过程生成器匹配（Latent Process Generator Matching）框架：给定任意状态空间 𝒴 上的时齐 Feller 过程 (Yₜ)，以及可测映射 Φ: 𝒴 → 𝒳，可直接学习观测空间 𝒳 上的 Feller 过程生成器，使其单时刻边缘分布与 (Φ(Yₜ)) 的边缘分布一致。核心贡献是将 Generator Matching 理论从对静态隐变量的条件推广到对隐随机过程的条件。当 𝒴 = 𝒳 × 𝒵 且 Φ 为第一坐标投影时，该框架统一了 Edit Flows、OneFlow、Flowception、Branching Flows、Kim et al. (2025) 等多项先前工作作为特例——这些工作各自独立推导了离散隐过程的投影公式。论文给出了一般情形下损失函数的有效性充分条件，并以蛋白质结构生成为例，展示了如何设计非投影型 Φ 映射来分离链级刚体运动和残基级内部柔性。","experiments":"暂无大规模实验结果。论文主要贡献为理论框架，第 4 节仅给出了蛋白质结构生成应用的概念性设计（未来工作计划实现）。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第 3 节 Pushforward Generator Matching 的定义和定理证明，理解如何将隐过程边际化并保持生成器的线性参数化。第 2 节对先前工作的综述可帮助定位该理论在现有扩散/流匹配谱系中的位置。阅读时间约 25 分钟，适合对生成建模理论基础感兴趣的研究者。","authors_note":"机构信息未在全文中明确标注。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Modern flow-matching and diffusion generative models often train with auxiliary stochastic processes (Markov processes on extended state spaces), but at generation time only need to simulate on the observation space. Existing projection results are derived case-by-case and restricted to discrete auxiliary states, lacking a unifying theoretical framework.","importance":"Provides a more general theoretical foundation for diffusion/flow matching paradigms, enabling the design of novel training schemes—for instance, using continuous or manifold-valued auxiliary processes rather than only discrete ones. This has theoretical implications for designing more flexible token-level generation (e.g., variable-length text, video frame insertion).","technical_approach":"The proposed Latent Process Generator Matching framework: given a time-inhomogeneous Feller process (Yₜ) on an arbitrary state space 𝒴 and a measurable map Φ: 𝒴 → 𝒳, one can directly learn the generator of a Feller process on 𝒳 whose one-time marginals match those of (Φ(Yₜ)). The core contribution extends Generator Matching theory from conditioning on static latent variables to conditioning on a latent stochastic process. When 𝒴 = 𝒳 × 𝒵 and Φ projects to the first coordinate, this framework unifies multiple prior works (Edit Flows, OneFlow, Flowception, Branching Flows, Kim et al. 2025) as special cases, each of which independently derived projection formulas for discrete latent processes. The paper gives sufficient conditions for loss function validity in the general case, and uses protein structure generation as a worked example showing how to design a non-projection Φ to separate chain-level rigid-body motion from residue-level internal flexibility.","experiments":"No large-scale experimental results. The contribution is primarily theoretical. This motivates a f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T07:06:54+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20813.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"扩散语言模型（dLLM）推理需要在每个去噪步重复做全量自注意力，且无法像 AR 那样用 KV cache，导致注意力开销随步数线性累积；现有稀疏注意力多在后期才敢启用、且以块稀疏为主，早期去噪一旦稀疏化就容易掉质量。论文要解决的是：如何在早期去噪阶段也能安全引入稀疏注意力，并把算法稀疏真正转化为端到端 GPU 加速。","importance":"对“非 AR 生成范式”的工程化落地更直接：它不改训练目标，而是抓住 dLLM 推理的结构性瓶颈（多步重复 attention）做可实现的加速。对预训练研究的启发主要在两点：1）dLLM 的注意力结构在去噪时间轴上有可复用的统计规律（相邻步相似、早期呈列稀疏），这类规律未来可反向影响训练（例如训练时加入稀疏/结构先验以提升可加速性）；2）提示 dLLM 的“早期敏感”并不等价于“必须全密集”，而是需要匹配正确的稀疏粒度。","technical_approach":"核心观察：早期去噪步的注意力并非真正稠密，高分注意力集中在少数 key 的“列”（column）上，呈现列方向的稀疏结构；而已有方法常用块稀疏（block sparse）在空间上做粗粒度近似，容易在早期误删关键连接，从而表现为“早期稀疏很脆”。\n方法 PulseCol：\n1）列稀疏注意力（column-sparse）：直接选择少量重要的 key 列参与注意力计算，而不是按块保留/丢弃。直觉是更贴合早期注意力的真实形态，因此能把稀疏窗口前移到更早的去噪步。\n2）周期刷新（periodically refreshed）：注意力模式会随去噪演化，若每步都重算稀疏索引会抵消收益；若全程固定又会失配。PulseCol 在相邻若干步复用同一组列索引，并每隔 R 步刷新一次以跟踪模式漂移，在“索引更新成本”和“模式失配误差”之间做折中。\n3）工程实现：提供列稀疏 attention 的 GPU kernel，使得列选择带来的算子级 FLOPs/带宽节省能落到 wall-clock speedup，而不是停留在理论稀疏率。","experiments":"论文给出的关键信息包括：\n- 在相同稀疏预算下，PulseCol 的生成质量-效率折中优于已有 dLLM 稀疏注意力基线；尤其在高稀疏率下更稳。\n- 80% sparsity 时，PulseCol 与全注意力相比“只略有落后”，同时明显优于既有稀疏方法（文中表述为 on average outperforms prior baselines）。\n- 结合自定义列稀疏 kernel，在不同上下文长度下相对 FlashAttention 的端到端推理加速最高可达 1.95×。\n（当前提供的全文片段未包含更细的任务指标/模型规模/步数 T/刷新周期 R 的具体数值表格；精读时需要从实验章节补齐这些可复现细节。）","reading_guide":"泛读优先抓三件事：\n1）引言里对“早期注意力并不稠密、而是列稀疏”的实证图/统计（决定了为什么块稀疏不合适）。\n2）方法部分：列索引如何选、如何在多头/多层共享或独立、刷新周期如何设定，以及索引更新本身的开销是否计入。\n3）实验部分：在不同去噪阶段启用稀疏的策略、与哪些基线比（尤其是后期才稀疏的方案）、以及速度评测是否包含索引刷新与 kernel launch 的真实开销。\n预计阅读时间：20–30 分钟可把核心思路与主要结果读清；若要复现/迁移到自家 dLLM，需要额外细看 kernel 与 profiling 设定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Diffusion LMs must recompute full self-attention at every denoising step and cannot rely on KV cache, making inference expensive. We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarch","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T17:59:52+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21489.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CV","stat.CO","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但常被忽略的问题：当扩散模型不再作为被训练对象，而是作为冻结 teacher 给下游优化过程提供梯度时，梯度本身是对噪声时刻 t 和高斯噪声 ε 的 Monte Carlo 期望。此时真正昂贵的往往不是一次 denoise，而是上游链路里的渲染、仿真、编码或生成器前向。若仍沿用训练 diffusion 时常见的朴素采样，估计器方差会直接吞掉算力预算。论文要解决的不是改 teacher，也不是改下游目标，而是在不引入偏差的前提下，重新设计 frozen source、synthetic signal source，真正的瓶颈常常变成信号估计的方差与成本，而不是模型表达能力本身。论文的贡献量级偏方法工程与统计效率，不是范式级变化，但它把一个分散在 SDS、蒸馏、归因等任务里的经验问题整理成了统一的 compute-aware variance accounting 框架，并给出无偏、可落地、可组合的降方差手段。对做 diffusion teacher distillation、score distillation、teacher-guided optimization 的人有实际参考价值；对更广义的预训练研究者，启发在于：当训练信号来自昂贵外环时，最该优化的往往是估计器设计，而不是继续堆样本数。","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"实验覆盖三类场景：text-to-3D 的 diffusion-teacher-guided optimization、one-step distillation、以及 data attribution。\n\n最核心的结果是有效算力倍率。论文报告 CARV 在 text-to-3D distillation 和 attribution 中带来约 2–3× 的 effective compute multiplier，也就是在达到相近方差时，只需原来约 1/3 到 1/2 的成本。这个结论和引言中的主张一致，也是全文最重要的实证点。\n\n在 timestep 采样上，使用显式 teacher weight 的 importance proposal，相比 uniform timestep sampling，在单次迭代成本相同的情况下大约再带来 1.2× 的方差下降。作者还用 toy analysis 展示 oracle proposal 相当于 3–4× uniform compute，而一个高斯峰值近似也接近 2×，说明时间分布选择本身就有不小空间。\n\n层次化重采样是主要收益来源。文中多处强调，缓存昂贵上游计算、重复采样便宜 diffusion noise，是 ECM 提升的 dominant lever。换句话说，真正决定收益的不是某个复杂控制变量，而是把“昂贵外层、便宜内层”的结构利用起来。\n\n值得注意的是，one-step distillation 场景里虽然方差/算力比改善明显，但没有带来下游 FID 提升。这个负结果很重要，说明估计器更高效不自动等于最终生成质量更好；可能是该任务已受别的误差源限制，或者训练目标与 FID 的耦合较弱。对读者来说，这比单纯报正结果更有信息量。\n\n从全文可见，作者更强调 variance-per-compute 和 estimator quality，而不是把最终任务指标包装成统一收益。这种实验组织方式是合理的，因为论文研究对象本来就是梯度估计器。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。\n\n第一，看方法图和问题设定，尤其是介绍两层随机性与成本拆分的部分。若只看结果表，很容易误以为这只是“多采样几次噪声”；真正关键是哪些随机变量值得重采样，哪些中间结果值得缓存。\n\n第二，看 Sec. 3.2 的 paper studies a practical but under-formalized issue: when a pretrained diffusion model is used as a frozen teacher, downstream pipelines consume gradients that are Monte Carlo expectations over timesteps and Gaussian noise. In many such pipelines, the expensive part is not the denoiser call itself, but upstream work such as rendering, simulation, encoding, or generator forward passes. Under this cost structure, naive sampling leads to high estimator variance per unit compute. The paper asks how to redesign the teacher-gradient estimator so that it remains unbiased while achieving much lower variance under a fixed compute budget.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense of improving diffusion training, but it is highly relevant to the broader pretrain-to-posttrain interface: once a large generative model becomes a teacher, reward source, or optimization oracle, the bottleneck often shifts from model quality to signal estimation efficiency. The contribution is methodological rather than paradigm-shifting, but it is useful because it turns a scattered engineering pain point into a clean compute-aware variance-reduction framework. For researchers working on score distillation, diffusion-guided optimization, or teacher-based distillation, the paper offers directly deployable tools. UI 或模拟器接口更值得读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets accurate simulation of modern LLM inference serving. Production serving is no longer a set of homogeneous monolithic replicas. Systems now combine prefill/decode disaggregation, attention/FFN disaggregation, multiple forms of parallelism, continuous batching, PagedAttention, prefix caching, chunked prefill, speculative decoding, and stateful workloads. Existing simulators often use averaged monolithic abstractions, which can mispredict latency, memory pressure, throughput, and even the direction of an optimization.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining research is indirect but practical. Inference is now part of the training loop for RL post-training, synthetic data generation, reasoning rollouts, and agent evaluation. A simulator that captures long decode tails, KV-cache pressure, phase-changing requests, and disaggregated execution can improve budget estimates for RL rollouts and help evaluate architecture choices such as MoE, long context, or speculative decoding under realistic serving constraints. It is a systems paper, but the feedback signal is useful when model design and post-training cost are considered together.","technical_approach":"Frontier is a discrete-event simulator built around a disaggregated serving abstraction. A control plane compiles a serving specification into role-specific cluster workers rather than assuming identical replicas. It explicitly models co-located serving, Prefill-Decode Disaggregation, and Attention-FFN Disaggregation, including synchronization and data movement such as KV-cache transfers. Runtime optimizations are represented as feature-specific adapters inside the scheduler–batch-engine loop, so they modify scheduler-visible state instead of being added as analytical correction factors. The workload model uses stateful requests that can include thinking rounds, tool-call delays, per-round token plans, and phase transitions, which makes it suitable for reasoning, agentic, and RL-rollout traffic. The design prioritizes closed-loop fidelity: queue evolution, batch shape, memory pressure, and scheduling feedback are treated as first-class effects.","experiments":"The paper reports that common analytical proxies can be substantially wrong. Token-count runtime proxies can produce 32.6% error on FlashAttention and 21.0% error on MoE GroupedGEMM. A memory model based on total memory minus weights can overestimate KV-cache budget by up to 27.2% and throughput by up to 32.4%. These errors matter because continuous batching, variable sequence lengths, KV-cache growth, and memory limits interact in a closed loop. Small per-operator or memory errors can move TTFT, TPOT, and throughput across SLA boundaries and change the selected configuration. The key experimental sections to inspect are the calibration against microbenchmarks or real traces, and the prediction accuracy for combinations of PDD, AFD, prefix caching, chunked prefill, and speculative decoding.","reading_guide":"A productive skim takes around 25–35 minutes. This operation can dominate runtime and, if implemented with unstable low-precision algorithms, can corrupt model outputs.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model research is practical and direct for long-context architectures. DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet, RWKV-7, Kimi Linear, and Qwen3.5/3.6-style linear attention models rely on related chunk-level triangular operations. The paper shows that an unstable inversion method can reduce Qwen3.6 MMLU accuracy from 75.6% to 0.2%. That makes the kernel a modeling and evaluation issue, not only an implementation detail. For long-context pretraining, such numerical behavior can affect loss spikes, stability under mixed precision, and reproducibility of training recipes.","technical_approach":"The paper analyzes both direct and iterative triangular inversion algorithms, with two constraints in mind: the algorithms should be rich in matrix products to use accelerator matrix cores, and they must remain numerically reliable in float32, float16, and bfloat16. The matrices arise from chunked Delta-Rule linear attention and are typically C by C unit-diagonal lower-triangular matrices, with C in {16, 32, 64, 128}. Their strictly lower-triangular structure is determined by key-dependent functions and causal masking. Rather than applying generic triangular inversion blindly, the analysis exploits the unit diagonal, strict triangularity, and small chunk sizes. A notable design point is that an unstable method such as MCH can still be useful as a subroutine inside a faster and stable method such as MXR.","experiments":"The reported measurements show that triangular inversion can take up to about 40% of a layer’s device runtime in existing implementations.
