是核心。实验部分优先看 ID group accuracy、OOD consistency、不同领域拆分结果，以及 AIR 与对称不变性正则的对比。如果关心 RL 训练稳定性，可重点看它如何把 auxiliary loss 接入 GRPO，以及该 loss 如何改变策略梯度更新方向。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work targets a common failure mode in safety alignment: the same harmful intent can be rejected in a canonical prompt but accepted when wrapped in adversarial wording. The key difficulty is asymmetric supervision. Some contexts, such as multiple-choice or rule-checkable formats, provide reliable feedback, while open-ended generations usually depend on noisy and gameable reward proxies.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model research is in the interface between post-training and training signal design. The paper frames robust safety as context invariance over latent intent, but makes an important correction: invariance should not treat all contexts equally when their feedback quality differs. This matters for RL-based alignment, continual tuning, and reward-hacking analysis, where low-quality reward channels can otherwise pull reliable behaviors downward.","technical_approach":"Anchor Invariance Regularization, or AIR, designates verifiable contexts as anchors and regularizes open-ended variants toward those anchors. The anchor target is stop-gradient, so the reliable branch is not degraded to match noisy open-ended behavior. The method is implemented as an auxiliary loss that can be combined with group-based preference optimization, including GRPO. A heterogeneous prompt grouping scheme places verifiable anchors and their open-ended variants for the same latent intent in the same training group, making it possible to estimate the anchor-open discrepancy during policy optimization. Compared with symmetric V-REx-style penalties, the method is explicitly directional: reliable supervision constrains noisy contexts, not the other way around.","experiments":"The evaluation spans Safety, Moral Reasoning, and Mathematics. The reported headline results are a 12.71% gain in in-distribution group accuracy and a 33.49% gain in out-of-distribution consistency over strong baselines. The empirical story is that AIR improves consistency across prompt contexts rather than merely increasing isolated reward scores. The paper also argues that the auxiliary loss suppresses spuriously high rewards on open-ended variants and encourages improvement when open-ended behavior falls below the verifiable anchor.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. Focus on the formal setup of latent intent, context, and per-context risk; the failure analysis of symmetric invariance regularization; and the AIR objective with its stop-gradient anchor. The practical details around heterogeneous prompt grouping and GRPO integration are the most relevant parts for readers interested in RL training dynamics. In the experiments, prioritize the tables on in-distribution accuracy, out-of-distribution consistency, and ablations comparing AIR with symmetric regularization.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不是在讲安全口号，而是在讲“噪声监督下别做对称约束”这个训练原则；只看方法动机和消融就够，若 setup 干净，值得迁移到 judge-based RL/SFT 混训里复现。","en":"This is less about safety branding and more about a useful training principle: do not impose symmetric consistency under asymmetric supervision quality; read the motivation and ablations, then consider reproducing it in judge-based RL/SFT mixtures if the setup is clean."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yixu Wang","Yang Yao","Xin Wang","Yifeng Gao","Yan Teng","Xingjun Ma","Yingchun Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["safety-alignment","preference-learning","invariance","reward-quality","stop-gradient"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20994","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.20994","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.21235","title":"LamPO: A Lambda Style Policy Optimization for Reasoning Language Models","abstract":"Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving reasoning language models on tasks such as mathematics, coding, and scientific question answering. solut","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T14:24:11+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21235.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是 RLVR 里一个很具体但很关键的问题：像 GRPO 这类 group-relative 方法把同一 prompt 下采样得到的一组回答压缩成均值和方差，再给每个样本一个标量 advantage。这样做稳定、便宜，但会丢掉组内回答之间更细的相对关系。对于数学、代码、科学问答这类稀疏 outcome reward 任务，很多错误答案都拿到同样的 0 分，只有少数正确答案拿到 1 分；即便都错，不同轨迹的推理质量也可能不同。标量归一化无法表达“它比谁更好、好多少”，导致 credit assignment 偏粗。LamPO 要解决的就是：在不引入 value model 的前提下，把组内 pairwise 排序信息直接写进 policy update。","importance":"从严格意义上说，这不是 pretrain 论文，而是 post-train / reasoning RL 论文。但它和基座研究并不远，因为它触及一个越来越重要的问题：当训练信号从 token-level NLL 变成 sequence-level verifiable reward 时，怎样设计更细粒度、方差可控、又不依赖 critic 的优化目标。LamPO 的贡献量级不在于提出全新 RL 框架，而在于对 GRPO 这一类实用目标做了一个结构化改写，把“组均值基线”换成“成对比较分解”。如果后续 reasoning-heavy mid-training、RL pretraining 或 outcome-supervised continual training 继续发展，这类 advantage 设计会有参考意义。它更像是对现有 RLVR recipe group-relative 变体，在不同模型规模上都能带来一致提升，尤其在数学推理任务上更明显。\n\n从全文描述看，作者重点验证了三件事。第一，pairwise advantage 本身有效，而不是仅仅因为加了一个辅助 reward。相关消融会分别比较：纯 GRPO、只加 dense reward 的版本、以及完整 LamPO。第二，置信权重和温度 τ 不是装饰项；如果去掉基于 log-prob 差的 weighting，或者把温度设得过低/过高，性能会下降，说明 pairwise 信息需要被平滑地利用，而不是生硬累加。第三，LamPO 在不同 group size 和不同模型上都保持较稳定，不是只在某个单点配置上成立。\n\n摘要没有给出完整数字，全文节选里也未包含结果表的具体值，因此这里不硬写不确定的百分点。但可以明确的是，作者把“稳定一致提升”作为主要证据，而不是单一 benchmark 上的大幅跳升。对这类 RL 论文，消融比绝对分数更重要：如果你关心方法是否可迁移，优先看 PDA、confidence weighting、ROUGE-L auxiliary reward、KL 系数和 group size 的消融关系。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精一点，建议先看 Method，再看消融，最后看主结果表。阅读顺序可以这样安排。\n\n先看 Introduction 和 Methodology 前半部分，确认作者批评 GRPO 的点到底是什么：不是说 GRPO 不稳定，而是说它把组内关系压成标量后，信息利用不够细。\n\n然后重点看 PDA 的定义式、confidence-aware weighting 的来源，以及 τ 的作用。这里要想清楚一个问题：为什么权重来自旧策略的序列 log-prob 差，而不是直接来自 reward gap 的大小。前者反映当前策略的相对偏好和区分置信度，后者则只是外部监督强弱，两者角色不同。\n\n接着看 auxiliary reward 部分。它不复杂，但要判断它是主要增益来源还是辅助稳定项，需要结合消融表一起读。\n\n实验部分优先看三类结果：主表、去掉 pairwise 分解的消融、去掉 confidence weighting 或调整 τ 的消融。如果时间有限，45 分钟到 1 小时足够抓住方法和证据链。若你在做 reasoning RL recipe，这篇最值得深挖的是 advantage 设计与 reward sparsity 的关系，而不是最终 benchmark 排名。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness in RL with verifiable rewards: group-relative methods such as GRPO reduce a sampled response set to scalar statistics like mean and standard deviation, then assign each trajectory a scalar advantage. That is efficient and critic-free, but it discards the relational structure inside the group. In reasoning tasks with sparse outcome rewards, many wrong answers receive the same score even though their reasoning quality differs. LamPO asks how to preserve intra-group ranking information in the policy update without introducing a value model.","importance":"This is a post-training / reasoning-RL paper rather than a pretraining paper in the narrow sense. Still, it is relevant to foundation-model training because it studies how to optimize sequence-level objectives when the supervision signal is sparse, verifiable, and delayed. The contribution is not a new RL paradigm; it is a targeted redesign of the advantage estimator used in practical critic-free RLVR. If outcome-supervised mid-training, RL pretraining, or continual reasoning training becomes more common, this kind of advantage construction is likely to matter. The paper is best read as a structured improvement to the GRPO family rather than a wholesale replacement of existing RL recipes.","technical_approach":"$3c","experiments":"$3d","reading_guide":"$3e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"RL后训练方法的扎实增量改进，pairwise advantage 的思路值得在内部RL实验中考虑，读方法部分即可","en":"Solid incremental improvement in RL post-training. r","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T21:23:24+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20506.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 用作人类行为模拟器时，训练信号不能只靠标量 reward。社交行为、用户偏好、人格一致性、保密性等目标通常是主观、多维、可解释的，单个分数很难告诉模型错在哪里、该如何改。Ditto 试图把 verbal feedback 作为 RL 中的一等训练信号，而不是只把它压缩成 reward。","importance":"这项工作对预训练研究的启发主要在训练信号形态。RLVR 只使用 task-specific judge 给出的标量 reward。Ditto 保留这个 reward，同时让模型在 rollout 后接收 verbal feedback，并生成一个 feedback-conditioned improved rollout。训练时将原始输出和反馈条件下的改进输出共同纳入 GRPO 优化，使基础 policy 上 Ditto 为 0.470，GPT-5.4 为 0.300。相对标准 GRPO，verbal feedback 在生成式和多轮任务上收益更明显，例如 ToMi +11 分、LifeChoices +11 分、TwinVoice +14 分。训练动态分析显示，Ditto 在 Sotopia 上从早期训练步骤就优于 GRPO，并且在 secret-keeping 维度上更稳定，标准 GRPO 在优化过程中更容易退化。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看 Method 中 verbal feedback-conditioned rollout 与 GRPO 联合优化的公式和训练流程图；再看 Soul 的任务构成，确认哪些任务有真实人类行为信号，哪些依赖 LLM 生成和过滤；最后看标准 GRPO 对比、训练曲线和安全维度分析。需要谨慎读的是 benchmark 和 judge 设计，因为人类行为模拟的收益很容易受评测器偏好影响。若关注 RL 训练信号质量，verbal feedback 的格式、来源、噪声控制和推理时蒸馏效果是最值得深挖的部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"For LLM-based human behavior simulation, scalar rewards are often too compressed. Social behavior, persona consistency, user variation, secrecy, and contextual appropriateness are multi-dimensional and subjective. Ditto treats verbal feedback as a training signal in RL rather than reducing all supervision to a single number.","importance":"The paper is most useful as a post-training signal-design study. RL with verifiable rewards works well when correctness is crisp, as in code or math. Human simulation needs explanatory supervision that says what went wrong and how to adjust. Ditto shows a practical way to distill such feedback into the base policy so that feedback is used during training but not required at inference. This is relevant to synthetic users, preference modeling, agent safety, and RL data generation, though it is not a pretraining-method paper.","technical_approach":"The method extends GRPO with feedback-conditioned rollouts. A task-specific judge or simulator provides both a scalar reward and verbal feedback after a rollout. The model then generates an improved rollout conditioned on that feedback. Both the original and feedback-conditioned outputs are optimized jointly, allowing the base policy to internalize the corrective information. The benefit is that verbal feedback can identify specific behavioral failures, such as being too polite, too predictable, inconsistent with a persona, or leaking secrets. The cost is dependence on judges that can produce reliable and well-calibrated natural-language feedback. The accompanying Soul suite covers 10 tasks across theory of mind, role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation.","experiments":"The main model is a Qwen3-8B Ditto policy. On Soul, it reaches an average normalized score of 0.726, a 36% gain over the base model, and matches or exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 tasks. Reported task-level examples include UserLLM, where Ditto scores 0.930 versus 0.575 for GPT-5.4, and Sotopia social skill, where Ditto scores 0.470 versus 0.300. Verbal feedback improves over standard GRPO most clearly on generative and multi-turn tasks: +11 points on ToMi, +11 on LifeChoices, and +14 on TwinVoice. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T03:06:36+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20643.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很关键的 post-train 问题：在 self-distillation 里，teacher 拥有 student 推理时拿不到的 privileged information，如何既利用这些额外信息提供密集 token-level 学习信号，又避免 student 被迫去拟合那些测试时不可见、甚至有害的 view-specific 偏置。进一步地，既然不同 privileged view——完整解答、部分解答、最终答案、hint、feedback——对不同任务和不同训练阶段的价值并不一样，为什么要预先固定只选一种？AVSD 试图同时解决这两个问题：一是多视角 privileged teacher 的融合，二是在“共享稳定信号”和“单视角特有信号”之间做受控平衡。","importance":"对预训练的直接影响有限，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口、reasoning 数据利用方式、以及 token-level dense supervision 的设计有明显启发。它的重要性不在于提出一个新的 RL 替代品，而在于把 self-distillation 里的信息不对称问题讲得更清楚，并给出一个可操作的多视角蒸馏框架。相比只靠 outcome reward 的 RLVR，AVSD 提供更密的局部学习信号；相比单一 privileged view 的 self-distillation，它减少了“teacher 过度依赖某种额外信息，student 学不会也不该学”的风险。如果后续有人做 reasoning pretrain、mid-training with solutions、或把 verifier / answer / rationale 作为不同 supervision channel 混合进训练，这篇文章的信号分解思路是可以迁移的。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把多个 privileged teacher view 的 token 分布拆成两部分：共识信号和残差信号。作者的直觉很朴素：如果多个 view 都支持某个 token，这个更新更可能稳定、与任务本身相关；如果只有某一个 view 强烈支持某个 token，这个信号可能有补充信息，但也更可能依赖 student 在测试时看不到的上下文。为此，AVSD 不直接平均多个 teacher，也不直接选最强的那个，而是构造两个 pooled target。其一是 geometric consensus target，用几何聚合强调“多视角共同支持”的 token；其二是 arithmetic marginal target，用算术聚合保留“至少有一个视角强支持”的 token。两者的差值可以理解为 residual，也就是 view-specific extra support。训练时，AVSD 不是无条件把 residual 加回去，而是通过 gate 控制：只有当不同视角在 promote-or-suppress 的方向上是一致的，并且 residual 的幅度相对 consensus 不失控时，才允许这部分额外信号进入蒸馏目标。这样做的原因很明确：纯 consensus 太保守，可能丢掉有用的补充信息；纯 marginal 又太激进，容易让单一视角主导更新。AVSD 试图在两者之间找一个受约束的折中。论文场景是 on-policy self-distillation：student 先生成自己的 rollout，再让带 privileged context 的同模型 teacher 对这些 token 给出分布，避免传统 offline distillation 的 off-policy mismatch。与已有单视角 self-distillation 的区别，不只是“多加几个 teacher”，而是显式建模多视角之间的一致性与分歧，并把这种结构写进 token-level target 里。","experiments":"实验覆盖数学和代码推理，重点是数学。文中明确提到在 math setting 里为每道题自动构造三种 privileged view：full solution、partial solution、final answer。模型方面，摘要和正文可见至少包含 Qwen3-4B。对比对象包括单视角 self-distillation 基线，以及 GRPO 这类 RLVR 方法。结果层面，作者报告 AVSD 在 AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25 等竞赛数学 benchmark 上优于单视角 self-distillation，也优于 GRPO。虽然当前给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，但从作者叙述看，收益不是来自简单 ensemble，而是来自对 residual 的门控使用。消融的关键看点应包括：只用 consensus target、只用 consensus target、marginal target、residual、gate 的定义与公式，这是整篇文章真正的新东西。读的时候要问两个问题：为什么几何聚合更适合表示共识；为什么 gate 的判据要同时约束方向一致性和幅度比例。接着看主结果表和消融表，尤其是不同 privileged view、不同 target 组合、是否 on-policy 的对比。若时间有限，30 看得更多”这一结构性矛盾。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a precise and important post-training problem: in self-distillation, the teacher has access to privileged information that the student will not have at inference time. How can one use that extra information to provide dense token-level supervision without forcing the student to imitate view-specific correlations that are unobservable, or even harmful, at test time? A second issue follows immediately: different privileged views—full solutions, partial solutions, final answers, hints, feedback—are not equally useful across tasks or training stages, so fixing a single teacher view in advance is an arbitrary and often suboptimal choice. AVSD addresses both issues by combining multiple privileged teacher views while explicitly balancing shared stable signal against view-specific signal.","importance":"The direct impact on pretraining is limited, but the paper is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface, to reasoning-data utilization, and to the design of dense token-level supervision. Standard sequence-level RLVR constructs positive/negative gradient centroids that can be dominated by high-frequency shared patterns (format tokens, problem entities), causing the implicit discriminator to overemphasize task-agnostic commonalities and weaken its ability to separate high- vs. low-reward responses.","importance":"Introduces a discriminator view of RLVR updates, reinterpreting policy-gradient updates as an implicit linear discriminator over token-gradient vectors. This provides a new theoretical tool for understanding token-level credit assignment in RLVR. The DelTA method consistently improves strong RLVR baselines without additional computational complexity.","technical_approach":"The key insight is that an RLVR policy gradient update acts as a linear discriminator over token-gradient vectors: positive and negative centroids are formed by advantage-weighted averaging of token-gradient vectors from positive- and negative-advantage responses, and a token's probability is increased if its gradient vector aligns better with the positive centroid. DelTA reshapes this discriminator by reweighting token-gradient terms in the RLVR surrogate, assigning larger weights to token-gradient vectors more characteristic of their own advantage side, making the discriminator more contrastive.","experiments":"Across seven mathematical benchmarks, DelTA surpasses the strongest same-scale baseline by 3.26 average points on Qwen3-8B-Base and 2.62 points on Qwen3-14B-Base. alignm","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T07:26:22+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20834.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论 Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T18:59:26+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20402.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"MXFP4 4位量化在 LLM RL 后训练中会造成严重精度损失，现有工作将量化误差视为单一噪声项，缺乏对不同误差组件如何影响 RL 训练不同阶段的机制理解，导致无法精准修复。","importance":"对 RL 后训练的量化落地有直接工程价值。将量化误差分解为三个可独立寻址的组件，为设计针对性修复策略提供了理论框架。尤其对正在转向 RL 作为核心训练阶段的基座模型管线，4-bit 量化的可用性直接影响训练成本。","technical_approach":"论文对 MXFP4 量化误差做了精确的三项正交分解：(1) 尺度偏差（scale bias）：E8M0 共享尺度因上取整操作平均超调理想尺度约 44%，在反向传播中乘性积累，影响梯度精度；(2) 死区截断（deadzone truncation）：块内绝对值小于最大值 1/24 的值被置零，约占 9% 的权重，在正向传播中影响 rollout 质量；(3) 网格噪声（grid noise）：E2M1 粗网格引入的零均值圆整噪声，提高策略的有效温度（增加探索），且该项与尺度精度无关——即使尺度完美也无法消除，构成不可约误差底限。三项误差中，死区与另外两项逐点正交，尺度与网格噪声呈负相关（cos ≈ -0.66）。针对每一组件分别提出修复：宏块缩放（macro-block scaling）缓解尺度偏差，离群值回落（Outlier Fallback）修复死区截断，自适应量化噪声（Adaptive Quantization Noise）控制策略熵。","experiments":"在密集模型（Qwen2.5-3B）和 MoE 模型（Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base）的 RL 后训练上验证，将 BF16 精度差距分别缩小到 0.7 和 3.0 个百分点以内。理论部分给出了精确的分解证明和 MSE 恒等式，对 18,876 个权重张量的实证测量确认了三项分量的正交性和负相关性。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第 3 节量化误差分解的数学证明（引理 3.2 和 MSE 恒等式推导），以及第 4 节三项误差分别对应 RL 哪条通路（正向/反向/策略熵）的机制分析。实验部分简洁，可快速浏览。阅读时间约 30 分钟。","authors_note":"机构信息未在全文中明确标注。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"MXFP4 4-bit quantization causes severe accuracy degradation in LLM RL post-training. Existing work treats quantization error as a monolithic noise term, lacking a mechanistic understanding of how distinct error components affect different stages of RL training, which prevents targeted remediation.","importance":"Direct engineering value for quantization deployment in RL post-training pipelines. Decomposing quantization error into three independently addressable components provides a theoretical framework for designing targeted correction strategies. For base model pipelines increasingly adopting RL as a core training phase, 4-bit quantization availability directly impacts training cost.","technical_approach":"The paper derives an exact three-way orthogonal decomposition of MXFP4 quantization error: (1) Scale bias: the E8M0 shared scale overshoots the ideal scale by ~44% on average due to ceiling rounding, accumulating multiplicatively across layers in the backward pass and degrading gradient accuracy; (2) Deadzone truncation: values with absolute magnitude less than 1/24 of the block maximum are zeroed, affecting ~9% of weights and degrading rollout quality in the forward pass; (3) Grid noise: zero-mean rounding noise from the coarse E2M1 grid, which raises the policy's effective temperature (increasing exploration) and is invariant to scale precision—thus irreducible even with perfect scaling. Among the three components, deadzone is pointwise orthogonal to the other two, while scale and grid noise are negatively correlated (cos ≈ -0.66). Targeted corrections are proposed: macro-block scaling for scale bias, Outlier Fallback for deadzone truncation, and Adaptive Quantization Noise for policy entropy control.","experiments":"Validated on RL post-training of both a dense model (Qwen2.5-3B) and an MoE model (Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base), recovering BF16 accuracy to within 0.7 and 3.0 percentage points respectively. Meanwhile, using full-trajectory likelihood ratios leads to excessive estimation variance and unstable training due to multiplication of many token-level ratios, creating a bias-variance dilemma in RLVR.","importance":"This work provides a principled, tunable knob for bias-variance trade-off in RLVR, which can reduce the structural bias of PPO-style methods without sacrificing training stability. It improves the efficiency of reasoning ability learning in the RL stage connecting pretraining and post-training, and offers a new theoretical and practical framework for RLHF/RLAIF algorithm design.","technical_approach":"Starting from the exact policy improvement identity, the work first proves that the bias of PPO's local surrogate objective comes from omitting the product of forward likelihood ratios for future tokens (full forward trace). It then proposes the N-step forward trace construction: for each token's update, only the product of likelihood ratios of the next N-1 tokens is retained, interpolating between PPO (N=1, no future correction) and exact policy gradient (N=T, full-trajectory correction). Theoretical analysis shows that increasing N reduces truncation bias at a rate of O((T-N)²δ²), while variance grows exponentially with N, making N a tunable trade-off parameter. Based on this, the N-step Forward-Trace Policy Optimization (NFPO) algorithm is proposed and implemented within a masked policy gradient framework.","experiments":"On multiple reasoning benchmarks, NFPO consistently outperforms or matches existing RLVR baselines including GRPO and DPPO. Mechanistic analysis shows that forward trace correction concentrates on reasoning-related tokens, and filters noise in local likelihood ratios by aggregating learning signals from future tokens to improve training stability. Experiments with varying N values also validate the theoretically predicted bias-variance trade-off relationship.","reading_guide":"For core insights, read Chapter 1 (problem statement) and Chapter 2 (exact policy improvement identity derivation), which takes around 15 minutes. The key issue is not only distribution shift from a cold SFT policy, but the lack of informative rollouts: for many prompts, all sampled solutions are either correct or incorrect, so preference pairs carry little contrastive signal.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining-oriented research is in the interface between pretraining, SFT, and RL-style reasoning training. A short online RL phase can be used as a distribution calibrator rather than as the main optimizer. Once the policy reaches a regime where correct and incorrect solutions co-occur, offline preference optimization becomes much more effective. This is relevant for scaling reasoning training because online rollout generation is often the expensive part of RLVR.","technical_approach":"G2D has three stages. First, starting from an SFT model M0, the method runs K steps of GRPO with LoRA rank 16, using G=2 rollouts per prompt and a binary Math-Verify reward. This warm-up is meant to move the policy into a more useful uncertainty regime, not to finish online optimization. Second, the warmed-up policy is frozen and used to harvest a static preference dataset with G'=8 rollouts per prompt at temperature 1.3, increasing diversity and the chance of mixed correct and incorrect samples. Third, a fresh copy of M0 is trained with DPO on this fixed dataset. The analysis focuses on rollout informativeness, measured through entropy and middle-band metrics, rather than raw preference-pair count.","experiments":"Experiments are run on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B with verifiable math rewards. The main empirical pattern is clear: moderate GRPO warm-up lets offline DPO match or sometimes outperform continued GRPO in the reported setup while requiring less online generation. Too little warm-up leaves the policy in a cold regime with many all-wrong or all-right prompt groups. Too much warm-up also hurts, because the policy becomes too deterministic or format-biased, reducing useful preference contrast. The optimal warm-up length is model-dependent and tied to verifier format compliance. The provided content does not include the complete numerical tables, so the result should be read as a recipe under the stated models, budgets, and GRPO setup rather than a universal scaling claim.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. However, GRPO is prone to advantage collapse, a failure mode where homogeneous rewards within a group (e.g., all correct or all incorrect answers) yield near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients. To address this, we introduce the Advantage Collapse Rate (","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T12:57:37+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21125.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作盯住的是 GRPO 在 RLVR 训练里的一个很具体但常被忽略的失效点：当同一 prompt 下采样得到的一组回答奖励完全同质时，组内标准化优势会退化到接近 0，整批样本几乎不给梯度。对二值可验证奖励，尤其是数学推理这类任务，这种情况并不罕见：要么一组全错，要么一组全对。结果是训练表面上还在跑，loss 和准确率也未必立刻异常，但大量算力其实花在了无效 batch 上。论文要解决的不是泛泛的 RL 不稳定，而是如何在不引入 critic、不过多增加系统复杂度的前提下，诊断并缓解这种 advantage collapse。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口很有参考价值，尤其是当推理能力越来越依赖 RLVR/RL 阶段时。它的主要贡献有两层。第一层是把一个经验现象变成了可监控的训练信号：ACR 用现成的 reward variance 统计就能实时估计多少 batch 实际没有学习信号，而且早期 ACR 对最终性能有较强预测力，文中给出 R²=0.617。第二层是提出一个很轻量的补救思路 AVSPO，不增加额外 LLM forward，就能把原本完全浪费的 batch 重新变成有梯度的更新。对做大规模训练的人，价值不在于它一定是最终答案，而在于它提醒了一件事：在 group-based policy optimization 里，'有没有梯度' 本身就该是一级监控指标，而不是只看 reward 曲线。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。先是诊断。作者定义 Advantage Collapse Rate，统计训练中发生完全或近完全优势塌缩的 batch 比例。因为 GRPO 的优势来自组内 reward 标准化，若一组奖励全相同，则均值等于每个样本奖励、标准差为 0，所有优势都变成 0；即便不是严格全同，只要方差极小，也会出现近似无梯度。ACR 的好处是实现成本很低，训练时本来就会算组内 reward 分布，不需要额外模型。\n\n缓解部分是 AVSPO。核心想法不是改 PPO/GRPO 目标，也不是引入 value model，而是在检测到 collapse 时，为该组注入虚拟 reward 样本，构造出非零的相对比较基准，让原本同质奖励组也能得到非零优势。论文强调这是 plug-and-play 的：不需要额外生成新回答，也不需要额外前向，只在优势计算阶段做调整。它和 GAE、critic-based PPO 的区别在于不依赖价值函数；和 PRM 的区别在于不需要过程级标注；和单纯 entropy regularization 的区别在于它针对的是 reward 同质导致的梯度消失，而不是泛化地鼓励探索。按文中的表述，AVSPO 更像是在保留 GRPO 低内存、无 critic 优点的同时，给 collapsed group 人工补一个最小可用的排序信号。","experiments":"实验覆盖 0.5B 到 14B 五个尺度模型，以及 6 个数学推理 benchmark。文中最关键的结果有三类。第一，collapse 很常见：标准 GRPO 下，28%–45% 的训练 batch 出现 advantage collapse。第二，ACR 有预测价值：训练早期的 ACR 可以解释最终性能方差的 62% 左右，说明它不是事后分析指标，而是可以早停或调参的在线信号。第三，AVSPO 的收益比较稳定：把 collapse 比例降到 11%–18%，相对下降 58%–63%；最终准确率比 GRPO 提升 4–6 个百分点；并且在 out-of-domain 的 MMLU-Pro 上没有明显泛化退化。\n\n从研究者角度，更值得看的是这些数字背后的含义。0.5B 到 14B 都出现类似问题，说明这不是小模型特有的探索不足，也不是大模型才有的 reward saturation，而是二值 verifier + group-relative advantage 这个组合的结构性问题。另一个细节是，作者指出常规 loss/accuracy 曲线对这种失效并不敏感，这和很多 RL 训练中的经验一致：训练看起来稳定，不代表有效更新密度足够高。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看引言和问题定义，确认作者讨论的是哪一种 collapse：不是 mode collapse，也不是 reward hacking，而是组内奖励同质导致的优势归零。第二，看 ACR 的定义和与最终性能的相关图，尤其是 Figure 1 一类的早期预测结果，这是整篇最有信息量的部分。第三，看 AVSPO 的算法描述，重点理解虚拟样本是如何进入优势计算的，以及为什么不需要额外 forward。\n\n如果只花 20–30 分钟，建议顺序是：Introduction → Preliminaries 中 GRPO/advantage collapse 的公式推导 → ACR 结果图 → 主表格里 collapse rate 和 accuracy 的对比。若要判断能否落地，再补看消融：不同模型尺度、不同 benchmark、以及 OOD 泛化结果。阅读时要特别留意一个问题：虚拟 reward 注入是否会引入系统性偏差，或者在高正确率阶段过度放大微弱差异。论文给出的答案偏经验性，若后续要复现，这会是最需要继续验证的点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a specific failure mode in GRPO under RLVR: when all responses within a sampled group receive the same binary reward, group-normalized advantages collapse to zero and the batch contributes almost no learning signal. This is especially common in math reasoning with verifier-based rewards, where a group can easily be all-correct or all-incorrect. The practical issue is not just instability in a generic RL sense; it is silent inefficiency. Training appears to proceed, but a large fraction of compute is spent on batches with vanishing policy gradients.","importance":"Its relevance is strongest at the pretrain–post-train boundary. As reasoning quality is increasingly improved through RLVR-style stages, the paper argues that gradient effectiveness itself should be treated as a first-class training metric. The contribution is twofold. First, it turns an anecdotal pathology into an online diagnostic: Advantage Collapse Rate (ACR) can be computed from reward statistics already available in GRPO, and early ACR explains a substantial portion of final performance variance (R² = 0.617 in the paper). Second, it proposes a lightweight mitigation, AVSPO, that recovers gradient signal from otherwise wasted groups without extra LLM forward passes. Even if AVSPO is not the final answer, the paper is useful because it reframes RLVR efficiency around signal density rather than only reward curves or final accuracy.","technical_approach":"$43","experiments":"The study spans models from 0.5B to 14B parameters and six math reasoning benchmarks. Three empirical findings matter most. First, collapse is frequent: standard GRPO shows advantage collapse in roughly 28% to 45% of training batches. Second, ACR is predictive: early-stage ACR explains about 62% of the variance in final performance, which makes it useful for online monitoring and early diagnosis rather than only post hoc analysis. Third, AVSPO consistently improves training efficiency: it reduces collapse to 11%–18%, a relative reduction of 58%–63%, and improves final accuracy by 4–6 percentage points over GRPO. The paper also reports preserved out-of-domain generalization on MMLU-Pro.\n\nA useful interpretation is that the issue appears across a wide scale range, so it is not merely a small-model exploration problem or a large-model saturation artifact. It is a structural consequence of combining binary verifiers with group-relative advantage normalization.","reading_guide":"$44","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇该看，因为它把 GRPO 训练不动的原因从“玄学”落成了可测指标；先盯 ACR 定义和相关性图，AVSPO 本身可先当工程补丁看。","en":"Worth reading because it turns GRPO stagnation from folklore into a measurable metric; focus first on the ACR definition and correlation plots, and treat AVSPO as an engineering patch until verified."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xixiang He","Qiyao Sun","Ao Cheng","Xingming Li","Xuanyu Ji","Hailun Lu","Runke Huang","Qingyong Hu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["GRPO","RLVR","advantage-estimation","reasoning","training-diagnostics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21125","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.21125","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.21127","title":"Reasoning-Trace Collapse: Evaluating the Loss of Explicit Reasoning During Fine-Tuning","abstract":"Explicit reasoning models are trained to produce intermediate reasoning traces before final answers, but downstream fine-tuning is often performed on ordinary instruction-response data that contains no such traces. We show that this mismatch can induce reasoning-trace collapse: a fine-tuned model continues to produce plausible final answers while losing the structurally valid explicit reasoning traces that made it a reasoning model in the first place. We introduce a structural evaluation framewo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T12:58:01+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21127.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 reasoning model 在后续 SFT 里的一个结构性退化：模型原本会先生成显式推理轨迹，再给最终答案；但如果继续用普通 instruction-response 数据微调，而数据里没有 reasoning trace，模型会逐渐学会直接给答案，显式推理部分变空、缺失或被截断。作者把这个现象定义为 reasoning-trace collapse。它要解决的核心问题不是推理质量高低，也不是 trace 是否 faithful，而是一个更基础的问题：模型是否还保留了“先推理、后作答”的外显行为，以及现有只看最终答案的评测为什么会掩盖这种退化。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不如训练算法类工作强，但对 post-train 如何不破坏已有能力很有启发，尤其是小模型 reasoning adaptation、蒸馏、持续微调这类场景。它的贡献主要在评测层。作者把 answer correctness 和 reasoning-trace validity 明确拆开，提出 valid / empty / missing / truncated 的结构化指标，并引入 reasoning-conditioned pass@1，去区分“模型会推理但推得不好”和“模型已经不再稳定地产生推理”。这个区分很重要，因为很多后训练流程只盯最终任务分数，容易把行为退化误判成能力保留。论文还给出一个实际结论：简单的 loss masking 就能在不少场景下保住 trace 结构，说明问题不一定需要昂贵的 teacher trace augmentation 才能缓解。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个结构化评测框架，而不是新的 reasoning 训练算法。作者先定义 reasoning trace 的有效性标准：生成中是否存在完整、非空、格式上可解析的推理段；无效情况再细分为空白、缺失、截断等。然后把这些结构指标和任务表现结合，计算 reasoning-conditioned pass@1，也就是只在存在有效 reasoning trace 的样本子集上看任务成功率。这样可以把两个因素拆开：一是模型在“有 trace 时”是否仍具备推理能力，二是模型“愿不愿意/能不能稳定地产生 trace”。\n\n工程上，作者实现了 ThinkPack，用于统一不同 reasoning model 的 chat template、trace parsing、validation、metric computation 和 loss masking。这个部分的意义在于，不同开源 reasoning model 的 trace 格式差异很大，如果没有统一解析层，很多评测结论会混入 prompt/template 偏差。\n\n缓解策略上，论文比较了标准 SFT、loss masking 和 teacher distillation。这里的 masking 不是生成新的 reasoning 数据，而是在训练时对 reasoning 缺失区域去掉损失，避免模型因为训练目标而被直接奖励“省略推理”。这和蒸馏的取舍很清楚：蒸馏更直接，但需要额外生成高质量 trace；masking 更轻量，适合私有数据或难以增广的场景。","experiments":"实验对象是 4 个开源 reasoning model，覆盖不同训练管线和不同 reasoning 格式；微调数据是标准 instruction-response 数据，不含显式 reasoning trace；评测任务包括新任务科学问答、数学推理和代码生成。论文的主结论是：普通 SFT 会很快压低 valid reasoning trace 比例，但最终答案性能在一段时间内可能仍然看起来还行，因此只看 answer accuracy 会低估退化程度。更关键的是，在若干设置下，conditioned on valid reasoning 的 pass@1 仍然较高，说明模型并不是完全失去推理能力，而是越来越少显式地把推理写出来。这一点把“能力退化”和“行为退化”区分开了。\n\n缓解实验显示，简单 masking 往往能明显保住 valid reasoning 行为，同时维持较强任务表现；teacher distillation 也有效，但成本更高。摘要和正文给出的表述偏趋势性，强调 collapse 发生得快、跨模型存在、且可被轻量方法部分缓解。若从研究角度看，最有价值的不是某个单点分数，而是结构指标与最终答案指标之间出现系统性背离这一现象。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，重点不在复杂算法，而在评测定义是否扎实。建议先看 Introduction 和方法部分里对 collapse 的定义，确认作者评估的是 trace 的存在性与结构完整性，而不是 trace 质量或真实性。然后看结构指标定义，尤其是 valid / empty / missing / truncated 的判定规则，以及 reasoning-conditioned pass@1 的计算方式。接着看主实验图表，观察标准 SFT 下 valid trace rate 和 answer accuracy 如何分叉变化。最后再看 masking 与 distillation 的对比，判断哪种缓解更适合自己的训练流程。\n\n如果只花 25 分钟，读法可以是：问题定义 → 指标设计 → 主结果图 → mitigation 表。若要复现或接入现有 pipeline，再补看 ThinkPack 的实现假设，特别是不同模型模板和 trace parser 的鲁棒性。阅读时要留意一个边界：这篇工作证明了“显式 trace 行为会消失”，但并不能单独证明内部隐式推理能力是否同步下降，因此不要把它解读成对 reasoning 能力本身的完整判决。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a structural failure mode during downstream fine-tuning of explicit reasoning models. A model may originally generate an explicit reasoning trace before the final answer, but when it is further fine-tuned on ordinary instruction–response data without traces, it can gradually learn to skip the trace and output only the answer. The authors call this reasoning-trace collapse. The central question is not whether the trace is faithful or high quality, but whether the model still preserves the explicit reasoning behavior that distinguishes it as a reasoning model in the first place.","importance":"Its main value is methodological rather than algorithmic. For post-training and continual adaptation, it highlights that answer-only evaluation can systematically miss behavior loss. The paper separates answer correctness from reasoning-trace validity and introduces structural metrics such as valid, empty, missing, and truncated traces, together with reasoning-conditioned pass@1. That separation matters because it distinguishes two different failures: the model may still reason well when it produces a trace, yet become increasingly unlikely to produce one at all. The mitigation result is also practically useful: simple loss masking can preserve explicit reasoning behavior in many settings without requiring expensive teacher-generated traces.","technical_approach":"$45","experiments":"$46","reading_guide":"$47","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把“推理能力退化”拆成可测的 trace 结构问题，能直接指导你检查 SFT/RL 是否在悄悄抹掉可控推理格式；只看他们的 validity 分解和 conditional 指标就够。","en":"Useful if you care about preserving explicit reasoning formats: the validity decomposition + reasoning-conditioned metrics reveal SFT can silently erase trace structure even when answer scores look fine."},"metadata":{"institutions":["King’s College London"],"authors":["Lukas Twist","Helen Yannakoudakis","Jie M. Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning-trace","fine-tuning","evaluation","chain-of-thought","distribution-shift"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21127","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.21127","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.21422","title":"Preference-aware Influence-function-based Data Selection Method for Efficient Fine-Tuning","abstract":"As LLMs continue to scale, improving training efficiency increasingly depends on using data more effectively. Data selection addresses this problem by allocating a limited training budget to samples that best promote a target behavior. Existing methods usually represent the target behavior with a set of target examples, but often treat these examples as equally important. This can be inefficient because target examples may differ in their relevance to the current model: examples closer to the mo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T17:15:43+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21422.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是预算受限条件下的 SFT 数据选择问题：当目标行为由一组 target examples 定义时，现有 influence-based data selection 往往把这些 target 一视同仁，导致构造出来的“目标方向”混入了离当前模型很远、短期内难以通过少量数据推动的行为信号，从而降低选数效率。作者要解决的是，如何让 target behavior 的表示更贴近当前模型的局部可塑性，而不是只做均匀平均。","importance":"它对预训练的直接相关性有限，因为场景主要是 fine-tuning / SFT repair，而不是大规模 pretrain；但它对数据效率研究有一个清楚的启发：数据选择不只是估计 candidate sample 的 influence，还包括如何定义 target direction。很多工作把后者当成固定输入，这篇文章指出 target representation 本身就是误差来源。这个视角对持续训练、mid-training 数据重加权、能力定向增益、甚至 RL 前的数据筛选都有参考意义。贡献量级上，它更像是在 influence-based selection 框架里补上一个常被忽略但合理的权重项，而不是提出全新范式。","technical_approach":"PRISM 的核心做法是把“当前模型对 target example 的偏好”引入 target direction 的构造。设目标行为由一组 paired anchors 定义，每个 prompt q 对应一个 target-positive 响应 y_q^p 和一个 target-negative 响应 y_q^n。传统做法通常会把所有 pair 的梯度差 g(q, y_q^p) - g(q, y_q^n) 做均匀聚合，得到一个目标方向，再用 candidate SFT 样本 z 的梯度 g_z 与该方向的对齐程度来打分。\n\nPRISM 认为均匀聚合的问题在于：不同 target pair 与当前模型的距离不同。若模型已经对某些正向 anchor 给出较高相对概率，那么这些 pair 对应的行为方向更接近当前参数点，少量训练样本更可能沿这个方向带来可实现的增益；反之，离得很远的 pair 即使语义上属于目标行为，也未必适合作为有限预算下的主要选择信号。\n\n具体地，作者先对每个 target pair 计算当前模型对正向 anchor 的相对偏好 π_q，即在正负 anchor 之间归一化后的正向概率。然后用这个 preference 对每个 pair 的梯度差进行加权，形成 preference-aware target direction。候选训练样本的分数则由其 influence signal 与该加权目标方向的相似度决定。高分样本表示：如果用它训练，参数更新方向更可能提升当前模型对目标正向行为的偏好。\n\n文章还给出一阶近似下的理论解释：在当前模型邻域内，PRISM 近似捕捉了单位数据预算下最能提升 target preference 的方向；而均匀平均会把高偏好和低偏好的目标混在一起，使方向偏离当前模型最容易被推动的局部行为模式。另一个实用点是，这个框架同时支持“保留有益数据做高效微调”和“移除有害数据做 SFT repair”两种方向，区别只在于如何使用排序结果。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类设置。第一类是能力提升型数据选择，目标行为包括知识能力、数学推理和安全 guardrail；第二类是 safety-critical SFT repair，即在固定移除预算下，优先删掉最推动有害行为的数据。全文主结论是：在相同数据预算或移除预算下，PRISM 选出的样本能带来更好的目标行为改善或更有效的安全修复。\n\n从论文给出的实验组织看，作者强调了两点。其一，效果在不同模型家族和不同规模上都成立，说明收益不完全依赖某个特定 backbone。其二，消融实验围绕“是否使用 preference weighting”展开，结果表明提升主要来自 target examples 的非均匀加权，而不是 influence scoring 框架本身。也就是说，关键不是换了一种更复杂的 influence estimator，而是把 target behavior 从静态平均改成了与当前模型状态相关的局部表示。\n\n你给出的全文片段没有包含完整结果表和具体数值，因此无法可靠摘录参数量、样本规模和精确提升幅度。能确定的是，作者做了跨模型、跨任务、双场景（retain / remove）的系统实验，并把理论主张和消融设计对齐了：如果 preference-aware target representation 真有用，那么在固定预算下应当比 uniform target aggregation 更有效，实验结论支持这一点。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读到中等深度。先看方法定义部分，弄清楚 target pair、preference π_q、gradient difference 和最终 scoring function 之间的关系；这是全文的核心。然后看理论部分，重点不是推导细节，而是作者为什么能把它解释成“局部最有效提升 target preference 的一阶方向”。最后看两类实验：能力提升和安全修复，尤其是与 uniform aggregation 的对比和相关消融。若你关心数据选择在 continual pretrain 或 mid-train 中是否可迁移，阅读时要特别留意它对 target anchors 的依赖，因为这决定了方法更适合有明确目标行为定义的场景。预计 30 到 45 分钟可以抓住主要内容。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies budget-constrained data selection for supervised fine-tuning. In influence-based selection, the target behavior is usually represented by a set of target examples, but those examples are often aggregated uniformly. The authors argue that this is suboptimal because some target examples are much closer to the current model than others, and therefore provide more actionable local guidance under a limited training budget. The problem is how to build a target direction that reflects the model’s current behavioral neighborhood rather than treating all target anchors as equally useful.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited because the setting is mainly SFT and SFT repair, not large-scale pretraining. Still, it offers a useful lesson for data-efficiency work: in data selection, the target representation itself is part of the problem, not just the influence estimator over candidate samples. That idea can matter for continual pretraining, mid-training data reweighting, capability steering, and even pre-RL data filtering. The contribution is incremental but meaningful: it adds a principled weighting term to the standard influence-based selection pipeline instead of proposing a wholly new framework.","technical_approach":"$48","experiments":"$49","reading_guide":"This is a skim-to-medium-depth paper. Start with the method section and make sure the roles of target pairs, preference π_q, gradient differences, and the final scoring function are clear. Then read the theory section selectively; the main point is not the derivation details but why the method can be interpreted as a locally optimal first-order direction for increasing target preference. Finally, inspect the two experimental settings separately: capability improvement and safety repair, with special attention to comparisons against uniform target aggregation and the associated ablations. If you are interested in whether this could transfer to continual pretraining or mid-training, pay attention to the dependence on explicit target anchors, because that limits where the method is naturally applicable. Around 30–45 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将 Influence Function 与模型当前偏好结合做 SFT 数据选择，逻辑很 make sense，值得关注其在实际 LLM 微调中的加速比和最终效果。","en":"Combining Influence Functions with the model's current preferences for SFT data selection is logically sound; worth checking its actual speedup and final performance in LLM fine-tuning."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Shanghai Artificial Intelligence Laboratory"],"authors":["Qihao Lin","Guanxu Chen","Dongrui Liu","Jing Shao"],"notable_authors":["Jing Shao"],"tags":["Data Selection","Influence Function","Efficient Fine-Tuning","Alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21422","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.21422","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20531","title":"Pseudo-Formalization for Automatic Proof Verification","abstract":"Reliable verification of proofs remains a bottleneck for training and evaluating AI systems on hard mathematical reasoning. Fully formal proofs, in languages like Lean, are easy to verify because they are unambiguous and modular. Most proofs, particularly those written by AI systems, have neither property, and translating them into formal languages remains challenging in many frontier math settings. Its useful idea is to replace direct holistic judging with a structured verification interface: decompose open-ended outputs into local visual claims, verify those claims against the image, and only then compose a scalar reward for RL. This pattern can transfer to multimodal instruction tuning, RLAIF, visual reasoning supervision, and synthetic data filtering.","technical_approach":"The framework still produces a scalar reward compatible with standard RL, but obtains it through claim-level comparison. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates concrete differences between the actor and the reference, verifies each difference against the image, and assigns side-specific typed errors. The reference is used as a comparison anchor, not as exhaustive ground truth. The same judge output supports two reward compositions. A relative reward compares actor-side errors with reference-side errors. An actor-only reward removes reference-side error counts and penalizes only actor-side errors on the discovered differences. These two choices expose different points on the faithfulness-coverage frontier.","experiments":"The main empirical finding is behavioral. Holistic rewards, with or without a reference, can reduce hallucination by making captions shorter and less informative, increasing omissions. ClaimDiff-RL provides a more controllable trade-off because it distinguishes unsupported actor claims from missing or correct details. The authors report that it preserves or improves captioning and VQA capability, suggesting that the finer reward does not simply overfit to a narrow caption metric. We observe that agents produce massive trajectories when solving problems, invoking tools and receiving environment observations across many turns. The evidence needed to answer the original question is thus scattered throughout these turns, requiring integration of distant context segments. Ne","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-21T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21850.png","hf_upvotes":57,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、但在 agent 训练里长期被忽略的问题：多轮 agent 轨迹里真正支撑最终答案的证据，大量存在于被 mask 掉的工具返回和环境观察中。标准 agent SFT 主要监督每一步的局部推理与工具选择，梯度大多停留在“下一步该调什么工具”这一层，难以把最终答案的监督有效传回到早期 observation 上。结果是，模型学会了局部决策，却没有真正学会把跨很多轮、分散在长上下文中的证据整合起来。ACC 要解决的就是这个 supervision blind spot：不增加人工标注，直接把 agent 轨迹编译成长上下文问答样本，让最终答案对中间 observation 形成直接监督。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发比较直接。它不是在做新的长文本语料构造，也不是单纯做 context extension，而是在利用 agent 运行时自然产生的大量轨迹，把原本只服务于 tool-use 的数据，转成可训练长程依赖能力的 supervision。价值主要有三层。第一，数据来源可扩展：搜索、SWE、SQL 这类 agent 都会持续产出轨迹，成本结构比人工长文标注更友好。第二，训练信号更贴近真实推理依赖，而不是人为拼接的长文 QA，因为证据本来就是在问题求解过程中逐步暴露出来的。第三，它说明 post-train 产生的轨迹可以反哺基础能力，尤其是 long-context integration，这对持续训练和 mid-train 数据回流很有参考意义。量级上，这篇工作更像一个高质量 recipe 和数据转化范式，而不是改变基础建模目标的工作。","technical_approach":"方法本身很朴素，关键在于它重新组织监督路径。作者先形式化分析标准 agent SFT：在一条 k 轮轨迹中，前 k-1 轮只对 reasoning 和 action token 计算损失，tool response o_t 被 mask，因此 o_t 对最终答案 y 的贡献只能通过很长的间接链路反传，信号会被中间多轮局部决策稀释。ACC 的做法是把原始问题、跨多轮收集到的工具返回、环境观察，以及最终答案，重新编译成一个长上下文 QA 样本。这样训练时，模型直接在“问题 + 全部证据 -> 答案”的条件下学习，原来散落在多轮中的 observation 不再只是上下文背景，而是成为答案预测的显式条件。\n\n从全文看，ACC 不是简单拼接所有轨迹文本，而是有几个关键约束。第一，使用 answer-verified trajectories，只保留最终答案可验证正确的轨迹，减少把错误搜索路径和错误工具调用写进监督目标。第二，覆盖三类 agent：search、SWE、SQL，分别对应网页检索、代码仓库浏览、结构化表查询，说明作者想证明这不是某个单一环境的技巧。第三，ACC 可以和现有长上下文训练方法叠加，它不替代位置编码扩展、长度 curriculum 或 packing 策略，而是提供更有针对性的 supervision source。\n\n机制分析部分也值得注意。作者观察到 ACC 训练后，模型在不同长程任务上会出现 task-adaptive attention restructuring，也就是注意力模式会按任务类型重排；同时 MoE expert specialization 更明显，说明长程依赖能力不是均匀地写进所有参数，而是通过部分头和部分 expert 的功能分化来承载。这一点比单纯报 benchmark 分数更有信息量，因为它暗示 ACC 学到的不是死记某类模板，而是改变了模型在长距离证据检索和整合时的内部路由方式。","experiments":"核心实验围绕两个长程依赖 benchmark：MRCR 和 GraphWalks。ACC 训练后的 Qwen3-30B-A3B 在 MRCR 上达到 68.3，相比基线提升 18.1；在 GraphWalks 上达到 77.5，提升 7.6。作者特别强调，这个 30B 级别模型在这两个任务上的结果已经接近 Qwen3-235B-A22B，说明通过针对性的长上下文 supervision，小模型可以在特定长程依赖能力上逼近更大模型。\n\n更重要的是，泛化没有明显以牺牲通用能力为代价。文中报告在 GPQA、MMLU-Pro、AIME、IFEval 上总体保持稳定，说明 ACC 至少在当前配方下没有把模型过度推向狭窄的“轨迹问答器”。这点对实际训练很关键，因为很多 long-context SFT 会带来 general capability drift。\n\n从消融逻辑看，全文最值得关注的是几类对比：一是标准 agent SFT 与 ACC 的直接比较，用来证明收益来自 supervision 重构，而不是单纯多看了些 token；二是不同 agent 来源数据的贡献，验证 search/SWE/SQL 是否互补；三是机制层面的 attention/expert 分析，支持“模型真的在学跨段整合”这一解释。摘要里没有给出完整数据规模，但正文明确这是基于 agent 轨迹自动编译而来，而不是人工长文标注集。若做复现，最需要补齐的是每类轨迹数量、平均编译后上下文长度、过滤后保留率，以及训练 token budget。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 和 Method 开头那段对标准 benchmarks，判断收益是否集中且代价可控。最后看机制分析，重点留意 attention map 和 expert specialization 的证据是否足够支撑结论。\n\n预计阅读时间 20 到 30 分钟。如果只想抓住方法价值，读损失分析、主结果表、一个机制图就够了。如果关心能否迁移到自己的训练栈，需要细看数据过滤和 trajectory verification 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in agent training: the evidence that actually supports the final answer is often buried inside tool outputs and environment observations, yet standard agent SFT masks those tokens from the loss. As a result, supervision mostly teaches local next-step reasoning and tool selection, not long-range evidence integration across many turns. The final-answer signal reaches early observations only through a long and weak gradient path. ACC is designed to remove this supervision blind spot by recompiling multi-turn agent trajectories into long-context QA examples, so the answer is trained directly against the scattered evidence collected during the trajectory.","importance":"Its main contribution is not a new pretraining objective, but a scalable data transformation recipe at the pretrain/post-train interface. The paper shows that agent trajectories, originally produced for tool-use training, can be recycled into supervision for long-context reasoning. That matters for three reasons. First, the data source is naturally scalable: search agents, SWE agents, and SQL agents continuously generate trajectories. Second, the supervision is structurally closer to real reasoning dependencies than heuristic long-document synthesis, because the evidence emerges through an actual problem-solving process. Third, it suggests a practical loop where post-training artifacts feed back into core model capabilities such as long-range integration. The contribution is best read as a strong recipe for supervision design rather than a change in the underlying language modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$4a","experiments":"$4b","reading_guide":"Start with the loss decomposition in the introduction and method section. selectio","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T06:09:41+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20767.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG","stat.ME"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章解决的是一个经常被忽略但很关键的问题：把 LLM 当作“合成人类用户”来做干预实验时，实验表面上像随机对照试验，实际上更接近 observational study。原因是模型会根据 intervention 的内容反向补全 persona 中未写明的潜在属性，导致 treatment 组和 control 组对应的并不是同一批隐含用户分布，而是发生了 user drift。这样一来，观测到的结果差异既可能来自 intervention 评测、产品试验、偏好研究、甚至 RLHF 数据生成。本文指出其中一个结构性风险：LLM 不是被动执行随机化协议，而是在做条件生成和语境补全，因此“同一 persona 在不同 treatment 下保持不变”这个实验前提并不自动成立。这个判断对任何依赖 simulated users 的 post-train 或 evaluation pipeline 都有现实意义。贡献量级主要体现在问题澄清和诊断框架，而不是新模型或新训练算法。","technical_approach":"$4c","experiments":"实验部分的重点不是追求某个 benchmark 分数，而是验证 user drift 是否普遍存在、negative control 是否能检出、以及 confounder adjustment 是否有效。作者在多个主流开源和闭源 LLM 家族上都观察到 confounding 证据，说明这不是某个模型的偶发现象，而是由生成式接口和训练分布共同导致的普遍问题。\n\n具体实验结构大致包括三类。第一类是 synthetic intervention setup：给定相同或相似的初始 persona，让模型分别与不同 agent / treatment 交互，再测量主要 outcome，同时额外询问或抽取 negative control 属性。如果 negative control 在 treatment 间显著变化，就说明存在 drift。第二类是偏差方向分析：作者展示 intervention-dependent shifts 既可能放大也可能削弱观测到的 treatment difference，因此问题不只是“噪声变大”，而是 effect estimate 方向都可能错。第三类是缓解实验：在 诊断，而不是普通相关性检验。第三，作者如何区分“模型回答风格变化”与“模拟用户身份变化”这两件事。第四，confounder adjustment 的实验细节：哪些属性被加入 prompt，为什么这些属性被认为与 treatment 和 outcome 相关。\n\n如果你做 synthetic data、agent eval、RLHF user simulation，这篇文章值得至少读方法和实验设计部分。若只关心结论，优先看引言、问题形式化、negative control 实验图表、以及 adjustment 结果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper identifies a structural flaw in LLM-based synthetic user experiments. These setups are often framed as if they were randomized controlled trials: the same synthetic user is exposed to different interventions, and outcome differences are interpreted causally. The paper argues that this is usually false. Because LLMs infer missing context from the prompt and the interaction itself, the intervention can change latent user attributes that were never explicitly specified. As a result, the treated and control conditions may correspond to different implicit user populations. The experiment then behaves like an observational study with confounding, not a randomized trial.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model research wherever synthetic users are used for evaluation, preference collection, agent testing, or data generation. Many pipelines implicitly assume that an LLM can hold a persona fixed while varying the intervention. The paper shows that this assumption is fragile because the model is doing conditional completion, not faithful subject simulation. The contribution is mainly methodological: it clarifies a failure mode, formalizes the bias, and proposes a practical diagnostic. That makes it relevant to post-training and evaluation design, especially when synthetic users are used as a substitute for human feedback.","technical_approach":"$4d","experiments":"$4e","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the introduction and problem statement first to understand the causal framing, then the section on negative control outcomes, and finally the mitigation experiments. Around 35 to 50 minutes should be enough for the main thread.\n\nFocus on four things. First, the causal graph and notation linking observed persona attributes, latent persona attributes, treatment, and outcome. Second, why negative controls are a valid diagnostic for drift rather than just another correlation check. Third, how the paper distinguishes a change in response style from a change in the underlying simulated user. Fourth, the details of confounder adjustment: which attributes are added, and why they are considered relevant to both treatment and outcome.\n\nIf you work on synthetic data, agent evaluation, or user simulation for RLHF-style pipelines, the methods and experimental design sections are the parts to read carefully.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看形式化和负控制诊断部分；它会改变对 synthetic-user 实验可因果解释性的默认信任。","en":"Read the formalization and negative-control diagnostic; it should lower default trust in causal claims from synthetic-user experiments."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Carnegie Mellon University"],"authors":["Victoria Lin","Taedong Yun","Maja Matarić","John Canny","Arthur Gretton","Alexander D'Amour"],"notable_authors":["Arthur Gretton","Alexander D'Amour","Maja Matarić"],"tags":["llm-simulation","causal-inference","user-drift","negative-controls","synthetic-users"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20767","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.20767","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20235","title":"Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine","abstract":"Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. The paper investigates whether GLU's edge comes from faster optimization or improved generalization, using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) framework.","importance":"The key finding for pretraining research is that GLU's primary advantage lies in accelerating training convergence (optimization), not in reducing the generalization gap. This conclusion informs architecture selection and training strategy design—GLU improves optimization via better spectral conditioning of the NTK, rather than through improved generalization.","technical_approach":"Analyzing two-layer networks in the NTK regime, the paper shows that the GLU-induced NTK matrix exhibits an approximate Hadamard-product structure: K_tilde ≈ K ⊙ XX^T/d, where K is the NTK of the corresponding non-GLU model. This structure reshapes the NTK spectrum with a more compact eigenvalue distribution and smaller condition number, implying faster convergence per established NTK results. The paper further connects this to gradient angle theory: GLU enlarges gradient angles between samples, making them more separated in gradient feature space, which accelerates optimization. For generalization, empirical comparisons show similar generalization gaps between GLU and non-GLU models at the same training error.","experiments":"Theoretical analysis is complemented by empirical verification on two-layer networks. GLU models exhibit faster convergence, including a characteristic loss-crossing phenomenon where non-GLU models have lower loss initially but GLU overtakes. For generalization gap (L_D - L_S), GLU and non-GLU models show similar values at equivalent training error, indicating GLU does not significantly alter generalization. The paper also notes a limited impact on final model performance.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 3 (NTK spectral analysis and Hadamard product structure) and Section 4 (training dynamics and loss-crossing explanation) for the core theoretical contributions. Yet the internal mechanisms that enable these iterative models to generalize beyond memorized patterns remain unclear. We hypothesize that generalizable reasoning arises from learning task-conditioned attractors: latent dynamical systems whose stable fixed points correspond to valid solutions.   We formalize this process through Equilibrium Reasoners (EqR), which enable test-time test-time compute（多迭代、多重启）稳定变强：哪些训练/推理设计会让潜空间动力系统形成与任务误差对齐的吸引子（attractor）地形，从而实现可泛化的收敛式求解，而不是靠记忆或外部 verifier。","importance":"对预训练主线（数据配比、scaling、优化器）不是直接贡献，但对“推理即动力系统/固定点求解”的建模范式有方法论价值：给出可操作的诊断量（fixed-point residual 与预测误差的相关性）和可扩展推理的必要条件（吸引子与任务度量对齐、深度-广度交互）。如果你在做 latent reasoning、any-order/iterative LM、或把推理当作可扩展的内部搜索，这篇能提供一套更工程化的分析框架与训练小改动。","technical_approach":"把 HRM/TRM 一类“权重共享的迭代更新”统一写成潜状态更新 z_{k+1}=f_θ(z_k;x)，将推理视为潜空间动力系统。核心主张是：test-time scaling 之所以有效，是因为训练塑造了 task-conditioned attractor landscape，使得稳定固定点对应有效解；并且 fixed-point residual ||f_θ(z;x)-z|| 可以作为与任务误差对齐的代理指标。\n方法上做了两件事：\n1) 机制与设计选择分析：从 feedforward 到 weight-tied iteration 的转变入手，系统讨论哪些训练/推理选择会影响吸引子可达性与“收敛=更好答案”的相关性，并用 residual-accuracy 的耦合关系做诊断。\n2) EqR 训练干预 + 两轴推理扩展：提出两种轻量训练干预来改善吸引子可达性——(a) randomized state initialization（训练时随机化初始潜状态分布 μ0），(b) path stochasticity via noise injection（迭代路径注噪，增加轨迹多样性，降低落入坏吸引子的概率）。推理时显式沿两轴扩展：深度 D（单轨迹迭代步数）与广度 B（不同初始化/噪声的多轨迹重启），并用“收敛度/残差”做轨迹选择或聚合，从而不依赖外部 verifier。","experiments":"主要在可控的结构化推理基准上验证：Sudoku-Extreme 与 Maze-Unique。论文报告 residual 与预测误差强相关（残差越低，解码错误越少），并观察到典型的深度-广度交互：只有当 D 足够大让轨迹能进入并稳定在吸引子附近时，增加 B（更多重启）才显著带来收益。最终在两轴扩展 + 基于收敛的选择下，EqR 达到 Sudoku 99.8% exact accuracy、Maze 93.0%（文中给出的代表性数字）。消融重点围绕：是否权重共享迭代、是否随机初始化、是否注噪、以及 D/B 扩展策略对收敛-准确率曲线的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约30-45分钟）：\n- 先看引言里“attractor landscape 与 task-metric alignment”的论点，以及 Fig.1（residual vs error 的耦合）和 Fig.3（深度-广度交互）。这两张图基本决定了论文的机制叙事是否成立。\n- 再看方法部分对两种训练干预（随机初始化、路径注噪）的具体实现位置：是加在 z0、还是每步更新、噪声尺度如何设、训练/推理是否一致。\n- 最后扫实验消融：重点不是绝对 SOTA，而是哪些设置会破坏“更多迭代=更好”的单调性，以及 residual 作为诊断量是否在不同任务/规模下仍稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"To explain and improve why iterative latent-state reasoners can reliably benefit from more test-time compute (more iterations and more restarts): what training/inference choices shape a task-conditioned attractor landscape in latent dynamics so that stronger convergence corresponds to lower task error, without external verifiers.","importance":"Not a direct pretraining-recipe paper, but it is useful if you care about alternative reasoning paradigms (latent iterative computation, fixed-point views, any-order/iterative LMs). The paper contributes an engineer-friendly diagnostic (fixed-point residual tracking error) and a concrete prediction (depth–breadth interaction) that can guide how to make test-time scaling behave monotonically rather than degrade.","technical_approach":"They model iterative reasoners as a weight-tied dynamical system z_{k+1}=f_θ(z_k;x). The central hypothesis is “metric-aligned attractors”: training should shape the attractor landscape so that stable fixed points decode to valid solutions, and the fixed-point residual ||f_θ(z;x)-z|| becomes a differentiable proxy aligned with task loss.\nEqR then adds two lightweight training interventions to improve reachability of good attractors: (1) randomized latent initialization (sampling z0 from a broader distribution conditioned on x), and (2) path stochasticity via noise injection during the iterative updates to diversify trajectories and reduce trapping in spurious attractors.\nAt inference, compute scales along two explicit axes: depth D (unrolled steps per trajectory) and breadth B (multiple stochastic trajectories from independent initializations). They select/aggregate trajectories using convergence (residual) rather than an external verifier, framing inference as an internal search over basins of attraction.","experiments":"Evaluated on controlled algorithmic reasoning benchmarks (Sudoku-Extreme, Maze-Unique) where memorization vs generalization can be separated. Key empirical claims: (i) lower fixed-point residual tightly correlates with lower prediction error; (ii) breadth helps only after sufficient depth enables trajectories to meaningfully settle into attractors (a depth–breadth interaction). With two-axis scaling and convergence-based selection, they report 99.8% exact accuracy on Sudoku and 93.0% on Maze. Ablations focus on weight-tied iteration vs feedforward, randomized initialization, noise injection, and how D/B scaling changes convergence–accuracy curves.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 min):\n- Start with the attractor/metric-alignment argument and the main diagnostic plots (residual vs error; depth–breadth interaction).\n- Check the exact implementation details of randomized z0 and per-step noise (where injected, schedule, train–test mismatch).\n- Scan ablations for cases where more iterations stop helping; those are the most actionable signals if you want monotonic test-time scaling.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"Zico Kolter 团队将 test-time scaling 解释为动力系统中的吸引子收敛，视角非常新颖，为理解 O1 范式提供了理论框架，值得一读。","en":"Zico Kolter's team explains test-time scaling as attractor convergence in dynamical systems; a highly novel perspective providing a theoretical framework for understanding the O1 paradigm. While task vectors offer a promising alternative by compressing demonstrations into compact hidden-state representations, their quality has been evaluated only through downstream task accuracy. This indirect criterion provides limited insight into how to design more effective task vector extraction methods. In this","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T05:26:38+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20730.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 ICL task vector 该如何评估、以及如何据此改进提取方法。已有 task vector 方法都试图把 demonstrations 对模型行为的影响压缩成一个隐藏状态方向或偏移量，再在 zero-shot 推理时注入进去，以减少长上下文 ICL 的推理成本。但过去主要只看下游准确率，这个指标太晚、太粗，既解释不了为什么某种提取方式更好，也很难指导方法设计。作者把问题重新表述为：如果 task vector 真的是 demonstrations 效应的压缩表示，那么用它做推理时，模型的 next-token 分布应尽量接近真实 ICL 的分布。核心问题因此变成如何定义这种分布对齐指标，并据此构造更好的 task vector。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响不在于提出新的预训练目标，而在于给“参数不更新的任务适配”提供了一个更可分析的中间层视角。它把 task vector 从经验技巧推进到可度量、可优化的对象：不是只看最终 accuracy，而是看它是否复现了 ICL 的条件分布。这一点对理解 ICL 内部机制、hidden-state steering、prefix compression、甚至 test-time adaptation 都有启发。贡献量级属于中等偏上：不是改写范式，但提出了一个相对干净的设计准则 d_NTP，并给出一个与该准则一致的线性方法 LTV，实验上在多模型多任务上有稳定收益，还额外讨论了跨模型迁移，这部分对小模型借用大模型的 ICL 能力有现实意义。","technical_approach":"方法分两层。第一层是评价准则 d_NTP。作者把 ICL 推理和 TV 推理都看成对同一 query 产生标签 token 分布的过程，然后比较两者 next-token probability 的差异。论文的核心判断是：好的 task vector 不只是让 argmax 标签偶尔一致，而是应在整个标签分布上逼近 ICL。这样做比只看 accuracy 更细，因为 accuracy 会把很多分布层面的差异折叠掉。作者实证发现 d_NTP 与下游性能呈明显负相关，说明它可以作为 TV 质量的代理指标。\n\n第二层是基于这个准则设计 Linear Task Vector。已有方法多半直接取某层 hidden state 差分、平均激活、或基于启发式位置抽取向量。LTV 的出发点更明确：把 demonstrations 对 query 表示的影响近似为一个线性映射。具体地，给定不带 demonstrations 的 query 表示和带 demonstrations 的 ICL 表示，学习一个线性回归关系，用闭式解求出能最好重构 ICL 效应的映射，再据此从少量 demonstrations 中提取 task vector。这里的关键不是“线性”本身新，而是它把 TV 提取写成一个最小化分布差异代理的回归问题，因此比简单差分更稳，也更容易分析。\n\n从机制上看，LTV 假设 demonstrations 主要在最后 token 表示附近施加一个近似线性、可迁移的偏移。这当然不可能完整覆盖 ICL 的全部非线性行为，但在分类场景下，标签空间小、决策边界相对局部，线性近似已经足以捕捉大部分有用信号。作者还把方法扩展到 regression 任务，说明它不是只对离散标签投票有效，而是对输出分布校准也有帮助。\n\n另一个有意思的点是 transferability。作者尝试把大模型上抽取的 task vector 迁移到小模型。这个设定隐含一个更强假设：不同模型的任务效应在表示空间中存在某种可对齐结构。论文结果表明这种结构至少部分存在，尤其对上下文长度或容量受限的小模型，外部注入的大模型 task vector 可以补足其 ICL 能力。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个分类 benchmark、5 个 LLM，并报告了分类与回归两类结果。摘要里给出的主结果是：LTV 相比已有 TV 基线平均准确率提升 9.2%，同时还能降低推理延迟，因为推理时不再需要完整 demonstrations。论文还显示 d_NTP 与下游准确率之间有较强负相关，这一点是整篇文章最关键的实证支撑：如果这个相关性不成立，分布对齐准则就站不住。\n\n从实验结构看，作者主要做了几类验证。第一类是方法比较：LTV 对比已有 task vector 提取方法，在不同模型和数据集上更稳定。第二类是指标有效性：比较不同 TV 方法的 d_NTP 与 accuracy，验证该指标是否真能预测方法好坏。第三类是泛化任务：在 regression 上也优于基线，说明方法不是只在分类标签受限时有效。第四类是跨模型迁移：把大模型提取的 task vector 用到小模型上，分类准确率还能再提升 6.4%。\n\n虽然给出的公开数字不算特别多，但从全文结构判断，真正值得看的不是单一平均提升，而是不同模型规模、不同 demonstration 数量、不同任务类型下 LTV 是否都保持优势。如果某些任务上 d_NTP 改善明显但 accuracy 改善有限，那通常意味着分布更接近 ICL，但 ICL 本身在该任务上也未必最优；这类结果对理解“模仿 ICL”与“提升任务性能”之间的关系很重要。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看引言和方法部分里 d_NTP 的定义，再看 LTV 的回归构造，最后看相关性实验和 transfer 实验。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线。\n\n阅读时重点看三件事。第一，作者如何严格定义 ICL 分布与 TV 分布的差异，尤其是标签受限 next-token 分布的处理方式。第二，LTV 的闭式解到底在拟合什么对象：是直接拟合 hidden-state shift，还是通过 LM head 间接对齐输出分布，这会影响你对方法适用范围的判断。第三，d_NTP 与 accuracy 的相关性图和跨模型迁移结果，这两处最能体现文章的独立信息量。\n\n如果只想快速判断是否值得继续深挖，优先看：提出 d_NTP 的章节、LTV 推导、主表、相关性图、transfer 结果。若你关心 ICL 机制而不是 task vector 工程实现，可以把注意力放在“为什么分布对齐比准确率更像一个正确的中间目标”这一点上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: how should task vectors for in-context learning be evaluated and designed? Prior work treats task vectors as compressed representations of the effect of demonstrations, so that one can remove the demonstrations at inference time and still recover some of the ICL gain. But existing comparisons rely almost entirely on downstream accuracy. That is too coarse to explain why one extraction method works better than another. The paper reframes the problem as distribution matching: if a task vector truly captures the effect of demonstrations, then task-vector-based inference should induce a next-token distribution close to the one produced by full ICL.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it gives a cleaner lens on test-time adaptation without weight updates. The main value is conceptual: it turns task vectors from a heuristic artifact into something that can be measured against an explicit target, namely the ICL predictive distribution. That matters for understanding ICL mechanisms, hidden-state steering, prompt compression, and possibly other forms of activation-space adaptation. The contribution is moderate but real: a useful metric, d_NTP, plus a linear extraction method aligned with that metric, with consistent gains across models and tasks and an interesting cross-model transfer result.","technical_approach":"$50","experiments":"$51","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the section defining d_NTP, then read the LTV derivation, and finally inspect the correlation plots and transfer experiments. Around 30 to 45 minutes should be enough to capture the main idea.\n\nFocus on three points. First, how exactly the paper defines the discrepancy between ICL and task-vector inference, especially under label-restricted next-token distributions. Second, what object LTV is fitting: a hidden-state shift, a linear map, or something more directly tied to output logits. That determines how broadly the method may generalize. Third, the evidence that d_NTP tracks downstream quality and the larger-to-smaller model transfer results, since those are the parts with the most independent signal.\n\nIf you only want the essence, read the d_NTP definition, the LTV formulation, the main result table, the correlation figure, and the transfer section.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不在于 LTV 本身，而在于把 task vector 评估从 accuracy 拉回 token distribution；若你关心 ICL 压缩，只看 d_NTP 定义和相关性图就够了。","en":"The useful part is not LTV itself but the shift from accuracy to token-distribution matching; if you care about ICL compression, read the d_NTP definition and the correlation plots."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Seoul National University","Yonsei University"],"authors":["Jihoon Kwon","Jiwon Choi","Jy-yong Sohn"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["in-context-learning","task-vectors","representation-analysis","distribution-alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20730","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.20730","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.21391","title":"Post-Hoc Understanding of Metaphor Processing in Decoder-Only Language Models via Conditional Scale Entropy","abstract":"Metaphor requires a language model to resolve a token whose contextual meaning diverges from its basic literal sense. Understanding how transformer models organize this reinterpretation across depth remains an open problem in mechanistic interpretability. We introduce conditional scale entropy (CSE), a wavelet-derived measure of how broadly transformer computation engages across frequency scales at each layer position. Two theorems establish that CSE is invariant to update magnitude, isolating t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T16:45:59+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21391.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究 decoder-only Transformer 在处理隐喻 token 时，残差流跨层更新的组织方式是否不同于字面用法。核心不是提升模型能力，而是提出一个可测量的解释性指标：conditional scale entropy，简称 CSE。","importance":"它对预训练研究的直接影响有限，但对机制分析有一定参考价值。CSE 试图把残差轨迹的跨层结构与更新幅度分开，因此可以作为分析模型内部计算组织的一种工具。对基座研究员更有用的部分，不是隐喻任务本身，而是方法能否迁移到训练动态、能力形成、层间协同和表示重排的分析上。","technical_approach":"方法从目标 token 的 residual trajectory 出发，即从 embedding 到各层输出的一串隐藏状态。作者先构造 metaphor-literal minimal pairs，再把每层 residual update 投影到一个归一化的平均差异方向 v* 上。这样高维更新被转成按层索引的一维信号。随后对这个信号做连续小波变换，得到不同层位置、不同频率尺度上的能量分布，再定义 CSE 来衡量某一层附近的计算是否分散到更多尺度。论文给出两个理论性质：CSE 对更新幅度不敏感，主要反映尺度分布结构；并且可用 majorization 关系刻画尺度分布的有序性。这个设计的目的，是避免把“大更新”误认为“更复杂的跨层组织”。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个 decoder-only 架构，参数量从 124M 到 20B，包括 GPT-2 系列、LLaMA-2 7B 和 GPT-oss 系列。数据包括 25 组受控 minimal pairs，以及 200 组自然 VUA pairs。主要结果是：隐喻 token 在连续层位置上产生显著更高的 CSE，并通过 cluster-based permutation correction。作者还做了 specificity controls：当句子只在语义复杂度或句法复杂度上变化时，没有出现同样的 CSE 抬升；matched-meaning triples 显示该效应更接近表达方式的 figurative mode，而不只是命题内容。leave-one-out 检查表明，用剩余样本估计的 v* 对 held-out pair 仍有正确方向的分离。论文也指出，在比较过的频谱量中，CSE 是跨模型和跨刺激设置最稳定的指标。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15–25 分钟。优先看方法部分：residual trajectory、mean-difference projection、CWT scalogram 和 CSE 定义。理论部分只需把握一个要点：CSE 试图消除更新幅度影响，关注跨层尺度结构。实验部分重点看 minimal-pair 设计、VUA 迁移、cluster permutation correction 和 specificity controls。若只关心预训练机制，可跳过大部分隐喻语言学背景，重点思考 CSE 是否可用于分析训练过程中某些能力出现时的层间协同变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether decoder-only Transformers organize their residual-stream computation differently when processing metaphorical token usages versus literal usages. The contribution is not a better language model, but a post-hoc interpretability measure called conditional scale entropy, or CSE.","importance":"Its direct impact on pre-training practice is limited, but the method is relevant to mechanistic analysis. CSE is designed to separate the structure of layer-wise computation from the magnitude of residual updates. For foundation-model researchers, the useful idea is not the metaphor task itself, but the possibility of using scale-structured residual trajectories to analyze capability formation, layer coordination, or training dynamics.","technical_approach":"The method treats the hidden states of a target token across layers as a residual trajectory. For metaphor-literal minimal pairs, the authors compute a normalized mean-difference direction v* from residual updates, then project each high-dimensional layer update onto that direction. This yields a one-dimensional signal indexed by depth. A continuous wavelet transform is then applied to obtain a scalogram over layer positions and frequency scales. CSE measures how broadly the computation at a given depth is distributed across scales. The theoretical claims are that CSE is invariant to update magnitude and ordered by majorization of the normalized scale distribution. This is meant to distinguish broad multi-layer reorganization from simply larger residual updates.","experiments":"The experiments cover five decoder-only architectures ranging from 124M to 20B parameters, including the GPT-2 family, LLaMA-2 7B, and GPT-oss models. The stimulus sets include 25 controlled minimal pairs and 200 naturalistic VUA pairs. The main empirical finding is that metaphorical tokens show significantly higher CSE over contiguous layer positions, with cluster-based permutation correction. Specificity controls show that the effect is not reproduced by literal sentences differing only in semantic or syntactic complexity. A matched-meaning triples experiment suggests that the signal tracks figurative mode of expression rather than propositional content alone. Leave-one-out tests indicate that the learned projection direction remains stable enough to separate held-out pairs. Among the spectral quantities tested, CSE is reported as the most stable across architectures and stimulus regimes.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–25 minutes. After normalization, the dynamics reduce to a two-factor product map with","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T15:25:13+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21292.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.AI","cs.LG","math.DS"],"summary":{"core_problem":"梯度流分析能解释简化 linear transformer 最终会学到 in-context linear regression，但不能解释有限步长、尤其是大学习率下的训练动态。该工作研究一个可精确化简的一 prompt linear self-attention 训练问题，分析有限步 GD 如何从收敛进入 catapult、周期、混沌或发散。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于提供一个可解的 transformer 训练不稳定性模型。它把大学习率下的 attention / bilinear 参数更新和已知的 cubic-map 相图联系起来，解释为什么梯度流看起来稳定，但实际离散优化会出现非单调下降、振荡、周期点和发散。它不能直接给大模型 recipe，但有助于理解 warmup、学习率阈值、参数化和稳定化技巧为什么会影响训练轨迹。","technical_approach":"从 Zhang et al. 2024a 的 one-prompt linear self-attention 化简出发，有限步梯度下降经过归一化后变成一个二维 two-factor product map。有效步长 μ=2η|y|√c，把原始学习率、prompt-response 尺度和两个因子的几何常数合并在一起。零训练误差固定点集合是 ab=μ，局部法向 multiplier 为 1-a²-b²，因此 μ 增大时稳定段缩小。作者进一步引入 error / imbalance 坐标 e=ab-μ、w=a-b。平衡线 w=0 是不变集，并精确退化为标量 cubic map，复现从单调收敛到 catapult，再到周期、混沌和发散的阶段。完整二维系统中，D=w²-(2-μ)(2-e) 的符号在 0<μ<2 时不变；D=0 给出一个不变椭圆，在该椭圆上动力学是 Chebyshev 映射 e+=e³-3e，也就是 e=2cosθ 时变成 e+=2cos3θ。这个混沌集合是 repelling separatrix，不是吸引子。对于 1<μ<2，平衡标量吸引集在横向上可以吸引附近的不平衡轨迹。mini-batch GD 被解释为多个 map 的随机切换。","experiments":"这不是经验型实验论文，主要结果是精确推导和动力系统分析。关键定量结论包括：有效学习率由 μ 控制；0<μ<2 时 qμ(e)=e²+μe+1 为正，D 的符号保持不变；D=0 上存在 Chebyshev 不变椭圆；1<μ<2 时，平衡吸引行为对附近不平衡扰动具有横向吸引性。平衡切片复现 cubic-map 的阶段结构，包括单调收敛、catapult 收敛、周期非收敛、混沌有界非收敛和发散。它提供的是机制解释，不是对真实 LLM 训练曲线的直接拟合。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30 到 45 分钟。先看 introduction 中问题如何从 gradient flow 转向 finite-step GD，再看 two-factor map 的推导、平衡线和 cubic map 的对应关系。若只关心大模型训练稳定性，重点读有效步长 μ、零误差曲线稳定性、catapult 区间和 transverse stability。动力系统细节较重，Chebyshev 椭圆和 D 符号不变性可作为机制理解，不必逐行复现证明。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks how finite-step gradient descent behaves in a simplified linear transformer when the learning rate is large. Gradient-flow theory explains convergence to an in-context linear-regression solution, but it misses discrete-time phenomena such as catapult dynamics, periodic behavior, chaos, and divergence.","importance":"The main value is a clean mechanism for transformer-like training instability. By reducing a linear self-attention training problem to an exact two-factor map, the analysis connects large learning rates in bilinear attention parameterizations to the cubic-map phase diagram known from quadratic regression. It does not produce a direct large-model training recipe, but it helps explain why warmup, learning-rate scale, normalization, and parameterization can qualitatively change training trajectories.","technical_approach":"Starting from a one-prompt linear self-attention objective, finite-step GD is normalized into a two-dimensional product map controlled by an effective step size μ=2η|y|√c. The zero-error fixed-point set is ab=μ, and the normal multiplier is 1-a²-b², so the stable part of the zero-error curve shrinks as μ grows. In error-imbalance coordinates, e=ab-μ and w=a-b, the balanced line w=0 is invariant and exactly recovers the scalar cubic map associated with the quadratic-regression phase diagram. The full two-dimensional system has an exact normal form. For 0<μ<2, the sign of D=w²-(2-μ)(2-e) is invariant. The boundary D=0 is an invariant ellipse, and on it the dynamics reduce to the Chebyshev map e+=e³-3e, equivalently e=2cosθ maps to e+=2cos3θ. This chaotic set is a repelling separatrix rather than an attractor. For 1<μ<2, balanced attracting sets can be transversely attracting for nearby imbalanced trajectories. Mini-batch GD is treated as random switching among related maps.","experiments":"The contribution is theoretical rather than empirical. coherence problem in mechanistic interpretability. Circuit discovery methods can identify sparse causal subgraphs, but the resulting findings often remain tied to a particular model, task, dataset, and intervention protocol. The goal is to give circuit findings a formal representation so that mechanistic claims can be compared, refined, and reused across experiments.","importance":"The contribution is not a pretraining method, but it is relevant to researchers who study training dynamics and emergent mechanisms. It provides a way to ask whether a mechanism found in one scale or task has a structurally and functionally related counterpart elsewhere. This matters for studying induction-style behavior, retrieval circuits, copying behavior, and reasoning-related components across model families. The impact is best viewed as an infrastructure proposal for cumulative mechanistic analysis rather than a result that immediately changes a training recipe.","technical_approach":"The method frames circuit interpretation as inductive theory construction. Each circuit is assigned a logical identity consisting of a Causal Functional Signature, an architectural signature, an ILP-learned theory, and validation statistics. The Causal Functional Signature describes what the circuit computes, grounding component behavior in causal attribution evidence over token positions and using two role levels: shared linguistic roles for cross-task comparison and task-specific roles for within-task structure. The architectural signature describes how the mechanism is realized structurally. It is learned with inductive logic programming over typed ground facts such as node type, layer, edge, component ratio, relative size, layer span, motifs, and normalized faithfulness. Unlike graph kernels or standard classifiers, the ILP representation is meant to produce explicit logical rules that support testing and θ-subsumption-based comparison.","experiments":"The evaluation starts from 15 circuits across 5 tasks and 3 models, and scales to 30 circuits per model across 10 tasks and 3 prompt splits. The reported result is that ILP architectural signatures achieve roughly a 3–4x structural separation advantage over Weisfeiler-Lehman kernel and random-forest baselines. The framework also records normalized faithfulness, comparing the circuit’s task performance against ablated and full-model performance. ada","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T14:51:20+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21260.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论 quasimetric 条件下，将 reasoning risk 分解为两个项：oracle-trajectory risk，衡量模型在真实轨迹最后一步上的答案误差；trajectory-mismatch risk，衡量模型答案导致 CoT 轨迹偏离真实轨迹后引入的额外误差。OTR 被解释为一个 domain adaptation 风险：真实 CoT 轨迹把测试分布 push-forward
