Existing memory-augmented agents typically rely on similarity-based retrieval from episodic memory banks or skill libraries, returning static entries that often misalign with the current context. In contrast, Mem-$π$ uses a dedicated language or vision-language model with its own parameters, separate from the dow","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T17:51:05+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21463.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agent memory 的一个老问题：现有方法大多把记忆做成检索库，推理时从过往轨迹里取相似片段，再塞回上下文。但 agent 场景里的“有用经验”往往不是一条静态片段，而是要根据当前任务、环境状态和执行阶段重新组织成一条短而准的提示。检索式 memory 容易出现三类失配：一是取回内容和当前上下文只部分相关；二是经验过于具体，迁移不到新情境；三是 memory 总是被调用，错误提示会直接干扰下游 agent 行为。Mem-π 的核心问题因此不是单纯“如何生成记忆”，而是同时学习两个决策：什么时候该给 memory，什么时候该 abstain；以及一旦要给，应该生成什么样的 guidance 才能提高最终任务成功率。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的论文，但对“参数化记忆”和“把经验压进模型而不是外部库”这个方向有明确启发。它把 memory 从 retrieval module 改成独立的生成策略模型，等于把一部分长期经验做成可训练的条件生成器。这和预训练里常见的外部检索增强形成了一个对照：不是扩大上下文，而是把可复用经验蒸馏进参数，再按需生成。更重要的是，它强调 abstention 和 counterfactual credit assignment，这一点对任何会向主模型注入辅助信号的系统都很关键，包括 tool hints、reasoning hints、critic feedback，甚至 post-train 阶段的 verifier/reflector。贡献量级上，它更像一个方法范式和训练目标设计，而不是通用基础模型层面的范式替换。","technical_approach":"方法上分两层。第一层是建模选择：把 memory 定义成独立参数化策略 π_mem，而不是 memory bank。输入是当前 context，包括任务指令 q 和环境观察 o；输出不是最终动作，而是一段插入到下游 agent 上下文中的 guidance m，或者选择不生成。这样做的直接好处是 memory 模块和执行 agent 解耦：可以用较小的本地模型专门学经验压缩，再服务更强的主 agent。\n\n训练分两阶段。第一阶段是 experience distillation。作者先构造离线经验库 E={(x,m)}，其中 x 是上下文，m 是从成功轨迹或经验总结中得到的 guidance。用监督学习让 π_mem 学会在给定上下文下复现这些可复用提示，相当于把经验库压缩进参数。第二阶段是 adaptation distillation，用 RL 直接根据下游任务结果优化 memory policy。关键点不在“用了 RL”，而在它怎么处理 decision 和 content 的耦合。\n\n作者提出 decision-content decoupled objective，基于 GRPO 做结构化对比 rollout。对于同一个上下文，会构造至少两类分支：生成 guidance 与 abstain；在生成分支内部，还会比较不同 guidance 内容带来的结果差异。这样 advantage 被拆成两部分：decision-level advantage 负责学习“该不该生成”，content-level advantage 负责学习“生成什么”。如果只把整个 memory 输出当成一个普通序列做 policy gradient，模型很难知道失败是因为不该生成，还是因为生成内容不对。这个拆分是全文最有信息量的设计。\n\n从系统角度看，Mem-π 不是让 memory 永久在线，而是把它做成一个可拒答的 intervention policy。作者强调在弱 grounding、模糊上下文、OOD 情况下，错误 guidance 会污染 agent，因此 abstention 不是附属功能，而是可靠性机制。和一些 always-on generated memory 方法相比，这里更接近 selective auxiliary generation。","experiments":"实验覆盖四类 agent benchmark：WebArena、WorkArena、LifelongAgentBench、ALFWorld，分别对应网页操作、企业工作流、终端工具使用和文本环境 embodied task。论文给出的主结论是，adaptive memory generation 在四个 benchmark 上都优于 retrieval-based memory baselines，平均相对 base agent 提升约 20%，其中 WebArena 上相对提升接近 50%。从任务性质看，这个结果说明生成式 memory 对长程、多阶段、上下文依赖强的 agent 任务更有帮助，因为这类任务里“经验重组”比“相似片段复用”更重要。\n\n消融的重点应当有三类。第一，是否需要两阶段训练。只做监督蒸馏通常能学到可读 guidance，但未必和最终任务成功率对齐；加入 RL 后，memory 会更偏向产生真正有用而不是表面合理的提示。第二，是否需要 decision-content decoupling。若去掉 decoupling，模型往往过度生成，或者无法稳定学会 abstain。第三，和 retrieval memory、always-on generated memory、以及不带 memory 的 base agent 比较，可以看出收益主要来自“按需生成”而不是单纯“多一个辅助模型”。\n\n从摘要和正文可见，作者更强调相对增益而不是超大规模绝对分数。没有看到它在参数量或数据量上追求极限，重点是 memory policy 的训练信号设计。若精读全文，建议特别留意不同 benchmark 上的触发率、abstention 率、生成长度、以及错误 guidance 对成功率的负贡献，这些细节比单一平均分更能说明方法是否可靠。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和方法图，确认它解决的不是传统 RAG memory，而是“生成式、可拒答的 memory policy”。然后重点看 decision-content decoupled objective 的定义和 rollout 构造方式，这是和已有 generated memory 工作拉开差异的地方。实验部分优先看三张内容：主表里的四个 benchmark 结果；abstention/always-on 的对比；以及 RL 与 SFT 两阶段训练的消融。若时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够抓住主线。若要继续深挖，值得问两个问题：一，经验库中的 guidance 是如何构造和清洗的，是否存在 teacher leakage；二，memory policy 学到的是可泛化经验，还是 benchmark-specific prompt pattern。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a recurring weakness in agent memory systems: most existing methods treat memory as retrieval from an external bank, but useful experience in agent settings is often not a static past fragment. It needs to be reconstructed into a short, context-specific hint conditioned on the current task, observation, and execution stage. Retrieval-based memory therefore fails in three ways: the retrieved item is only partially relevant, it is too specific to transfer, or it is injected even when no memory should be used. Mem-π frames the problem as a joint policy over two decisions: when to provide memory and when to abstain, and what guidance to generate if memory should be provided at all.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it turns reusable experience into a parametric generator rather than an ever-growing external store. That is a meaningful contrast to retrieval-heavy augmentation: instead of expanding context with stored trajectories, it compresses experience into model parameters and regenerates task-adaptive hints on demand. The abstention mechanism and the counterfactual credit assignment are also broadly useful ideas for any auxiliary signal injected into a stronger model, including tool hints, reasoning hints, critic feedback, or verifier outputs. The contribution is best read as a training-objective and systems-design idea, not as a new base-model paradigm.","technical_approach":"$54","experiments":"$55","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the method overview to see that this is not standard RAG-style memory, but a generative memory policy with abstention. In the experiments, prioritize the main benchmark table, the comparison between abstention and always-on generation, and the ablation on the two-stage SFT+RL pipeline. A 30–40 minute skim is enough to capture the main idea. Whether such experience can be distilled into reusable lessons that improve performance on future unseen tasks remains unclear. We address this problem by introducing the In-context Training (ICT) task, a framework for evaluating cross-task self-improvement in language agents. In ICT, a reflector model observes trajectories collected by ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T20:41:12+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20477.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有语言 Agent 的 reflection 多用于同一任务实例内的自我纠错，缺少对“经验能否沉淀成可迁移策略”的评测和训练框架。这项工作把问题改成跨任务在线改进：Actor 每轮只能尝试一批新的未见任务，Reflector 必须根据过去轨迹生成新的 system prompt，让 Actor 在未来未见任务上表现更好。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在模型规模或数据配比，而在 post-train 与 test-time learning 接口。它把“从交互经验中学习”从单实例 retry 变成可评测的跨实例能力，并把自然语言 prompt 当作一种可检查的外部记忆和策略参数。这个设置对研究 Agent 持续学习、反思数据合成、RL 训练信号设计有参考意义，但离基座预训练 recipe 还有距离，适合泛读。","technical_approach":"方法包含两层。第一层是 ICT，即 In-context Training 任务定义。Actor 按当前 system prompt 与环境交互，收集成功和失败轨迹；Reflector 读取当前 prompt 和这些轨迹，输出下一轮 system prompt。关键约束是下一轮面对新任务，不能通过重复同一实例来纠错。第二层是训练 Reflector 的 RL pipeline。训练信号来自新 prompt 在后续任务 batch 上带来的 Actor 成功率变化，而不是人工写好的 reflection 示例。与 Reflexion 这类方法相比，差异在于优化目标从“修正下一次同题尝试”变为“提炼可跨任务使用的策略说明”。MetaGym 则把普通 gym-like 环境包装成 meta-environment，用来构造这种多轮经验—反思—新任务循环。","experiments":"实验主要在 ALFWorld 和 MiniHack 上验证。ALFWorld 覆盖家庭环境中的文字交互任务，MiniHack 更接近程序化生成的探索和解谜环境。结果显示，经过训练的 Reflector 能在未见 task types 上提升 Actor 表现，并且在少量经验后生成更有效的 system prompt。文中还报告了跨 benchmark 或跨环境迁移的案例，说明 learned reflection 不完全依赖单一环境模板。更有信息量的实验点是消融：普通自反思或未训练 Reflector 难以稳定产生跨任务收益；直接依赖标量成功率训练的策略不如显式生成可读 prompt 那样容易迁移和诊断。由于给定内容中未提供完整表格数字，具体成功率提升需要查实验表。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。先看 ICT 定义和 Figure 1，确认它如何避免同题 retry 带来的评测泄漏；再看 Reflector 训练流程，重点关注 reward 如何从未来任务表现回传到 prompt 生成；最后看 ALFWorld、MiniHack 的未见任务划分和消融实验。值得深挖的是两个问题：一是生成的 prompt 是否真的学到抽象策略，还是记住 benchmark 风格；二是这种自然语言外部策略能否与参数更新、memory buffer 或 offline RL 数据结合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current reflection-based language agents mostly improve by retrying the same task instance. This work asks a sharper question: can an agent convert past interaction trajectories into reusable lessons that improve performance on future unseen tasks? The proposed ICT setting forces the actor to face new task batches each round, while a reflector updates the system prompt from previous experience.","importance":"The main relevance is at the interface between post-training, agent learning, and test-time adaptation. It treats natural-language prompts as inspectable policy parameters and provides a controlled way to evaluate whether experience can transfer across task instances. This is useful for studying continual agent learning and RL-based reflection data generation, though it is not a core pretraining recipe paper.","technical_approach":"The framework has two components. ICT defines a dual-model loop: an actor interacts with environments under a system prompt, trajectories are collected, and a reflector produces a revised prompt for the next batch of unseen tasks. The training pipeline then optimizes the reflector from experience without human-written reflection targets. The reward is tied to whether the generated prompt improves the actor on later tasks. Compared with Reflexion-style methods, the target is not same-instance self-correction but cross-task strategy extraction. MetaGym is introduced as a library for turning gym-like environments into meta-environments suitable for this loop.","experiments":"The empirical evaluation uses ALFWorld and MiniHack. The trained reflector improves actor performance on unseen task types after observing a small number of trajectories, and the paper reports cases of transfer beyond the training benchmark. The informative comparisons are against untrained or generic reflection baselines, which are less reliable at producing prompts that generalize across tasks. The supplied content does not include the full numerical tables, so exact success-rate deltas should be checked in the experiment section.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Read the ICT task definition and Figure 1 first, because the evaluation protocol is the main contribution.
